1a1(rgyud): @//rgyud na pa bzhugs/
1b1(rgyud): @#//rgyab/ras dang /
2a1(rgyud): @##/ O /shI DA karba Na ba ma hA yo gi ni tran+t+ra rA dza/bod skad du/dpal mkha'      
2a2(rgyud): 'gro rgya mtsho rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud kyi rgyal po zhes bya ba/oM sangs rgyas dpa' bo la phyag 'tshal lo//bcom 
2a3(rgyud): ldan 'das gsung gi mkha' bdag la phyag 'tshal lo/'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/bcom 
2a4(rgyud): ldan 'das dpa' bo chen po'i dbang phyug de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi rnal 'byor ma 
2a5(rgyud): rnams kyi b+ha ga rnams su rtsen (ba)dang      ldan par gyur to///der dpa' bo chen po'i dbang phyug gis bka' stsal
2b1(rgyud): pa||bde ba chen po'i ting 'dzin gyis/dbang po'i dra ba bdag gis mthong /'khor ba dang ni nges 'byung gis//
2b2(rgyud): mya ngan 'das pa so sor bskyed//de'i dbus su sku chen po//bdag gi dbang po yis rig go//dpa' bo rang gi rang bzhin 
2b3(rgyud): ye shes rgya mtshor mnyam par gyis/rnal 'byor ma 'khor lo dbus su//phag mo bdag ni 'dri ba'o//dbang po'i 
2b4(rgyud): dra ba ci ru bshad//bdag gis gang du shes par bya//mthong ba dang ni chen po'i sgra//bde ba za ba de bzhin ci//ting 
2b5(rgyud): 'dzin ming ci de la ni//byung ba nyams par byas pa dang //gang du thims par 'gyur ba yin//bcom ldan mya ngan 'das 
2b6(rgyud): 'khor ba yang dag byung ba ni//gang du thims par gyur ba yin//bcom ldan
3a1(rgyud): pa'i ming //ji ltar slar yang yongs su bskyed//skur brjod pa ni bsung bar mdzod//nyid kyi dbang pos 
3a2(rgyud): rig pa ni/bde ba chen por bzhugs gyur pa'i//bdag gi bdag pos gsung bar mdzod//dpa' bo'i dbang phyug chen pos bka' 
3a3(rgyud): stsal pa//phag mo lha rdzas pad ma can//lha mo rtse gcig yid kyis nyon//sngon du spyod pa'i mtshan nyid ni//mdor bsdus pa 
3a4(rgyud): yis bshad par bya//dbang po 'jig rten mchog chos bshad//dra ba de la yid rol pa'o//bdag gi nga rgyal tsam gyis ni//dam tshig mchog 
3a5(rgyud): gang byung ba'o//de phyir ye shes che mthong ste//thabs chen bde la zhugs pas so//gang du dbang po rnams kyis za//de yis nga'i ming sngon song //
3b1(rgyud): de tshe tinge 'dzin tsam du//thub (pa)skyes mchog rnams kyis gsungs//yang dag lhag rtog ye shes ni//sangs rgyas rnams kyi lha'i dbang pos//de rnaMs 
3b2(rgyud): su byung bas sbyor bas ni//mkha' 'gro mchog mang gis bya'o//bde dang mi bde gnyis mnyam las//slar yang 'dir ni byung ba ste//sgra ni gzugs
3b3(rgyud): las byung bzhin mthon //dam tshig spyod pa'i spyod yul lo//sems gsum rnams kyi dbye ba yis//bsgrub mo khyod kyis shes par bya//'di ni rang 
3b4(rgyud): 'byung bcom ldan 'das//dpa' bo mkha' 'gro dra ba'i sdom//'byung 'byung ba'i dus su ni//yongs su gsal ba 'od gsal ba'o//de phyir 'jig rten 
3b5(rgyud): 'khor byed//de ni 'khor bar yongasu brjod //'dir ni sgyu ma skye de bzhin//de ni 'thim pa'i dngos po 'ang med//mya 'das gzhan ba'i rdzas yod yin/
4a1(rgyud): @#/ O /'khor ba yi ni phyi rol son/srid pa'i rtse mo mchog 'dzin pa//de 'dir mya ngan 'das par brjod//gzugs ni sgyu ma lta bu'i sku/
4a2(rgyud): de'i gnas la rnam par 'phro//bdag chen ye shes gzugs kyis ni//dbang pos gsal bar rig pa ste//lha dang sbyin byed la sogs pas//mi mthong mkha' (la)'gro 
4a3(rgyud): ba'i gzugs//lha khyod kyis ni gsung rnams kyi//ye shes chen po'i ros bdag tshim//de ring bdag la the tshom mchis/dpa' bo rnams kyi rang bzhin ci//
4a4(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//dpa' rnams thams cad rang bzhin te//rang bzhin de rnams chos can no//'dir ni 'dod pa'i dus brjod de//'dod   
4a5(rgyud): pas sems las yongs su byung /'dod dang sems gnyis dag las ni//byung ba gzugs ni gtso bo'o//'jig rten gsum du rnyed dka' ba//thog ma dbus 
4a6(rgyud): mtha' yang dag gnas//gang zhig mtha' ma rnams kyi sems//sems de ro mnyam gyur pa'o//dkyil 'khor chen po'i sbyor ba bas/mkha' 'gro de ni de phyir 'byung ;
4b1(rgyud): rdo rje mkha' 'gro bdag nyid che//sa gsum po ni sgrub par bya/gang tshe sngon du yang dag 'byung /'gro ba'i gtso bo tshangs pa'o//gnas dang de bzhin ji ltar 
4b2(rgyud): stong //mgon po sems can rnams grol byed//de tshe 'jig rten khams gzhan gyis//chung du 'dir ni bskul ba'o//gson gson byang chung spros son 
4b3(rgyud): pa||sems can bsgral ba'i sems med pa//sgyu ma'i rang bzhin khyod bzhugs nas//khams gsum ma lus bsgral bar mdzod//yon tan ngag 
4b4(rgyud): 'di thos nas ni//sems can grol byed 'di ltar sgom//de bzhin gshegs pa kun rnams pa//'gro ba'i gtso bo he ru ka/dpal ni sngon du sngon brjod pas//
4b5(rgyud): rten la sogs pa bskyed pa ste//klu dang rol mo dri za dang //gzhan rnams gang rnams de rnams dang //'gro ba'i sde dang las dang ni//de yi rgyun las yongs su 'byung /
4b6(rgyud): de la sogs pa mtha' med dang /stong phrag sum bcu rtsa gsum mo//gsang ba mchog gis dges pa na/thams cad bdag nyid rtag     
4b7(rgyud): tu bzhugs//de nyid rnam pa bcu drug dang //skye ba dang ni thims pa'i rgyu//rnal 'byor rnal 'byed ma nyid dang /zla ba'i dbye bas 'gro ba'o//
5a1(rgyud): @#/ O /dga' ba rtag tu shes byas nas//tshe(sa) gcig zla ba 'phel bas so//nag po sogs dang dkar mthar yang //thung du de bzhin 'dir shes bya//
5a2(rgyud): 'di skad thos pa bdag gi dag//gsang ba'i 'dod pa thams cad ster//de bzhin 'dir bdag the tshom mchis/gsang ba la sogs gsung ni ci//bdag gi 
5a3(rgyud): bdag pos gsung par mdzod//sems can rnams kyi don byed pa//lha mo chen mo bdag dga' bo//sems can kun gyi dgos pa'i rgyur//mdor bsdus bdag 
5a4(rgyud): gis bshad byas ste//tse gcig pa'i sems kyis nyon//gsang ba khams thams cad kyi mtham//dbang po'i gnas ni mchog yin la//rnam shes phun sum   
5a5(rgyud): tshogs rnamasu//dges pa namakha' las byung ba'o//thams cad bdag nyid khams gsum gyi//khyab bya 'i gzugasu yang dag son//rtag bzhugs gnyis   
5a6(rgyud): su med ba'i chos//sgyu ma rmi lam lta bu machog/yang na dag med bcu drug dang //khyad par du yang snying rje'o//de yi rnam pa ye shes che//de kho na nyid kun   
5a7(rgyud): tu so na//'dir ni skye bar go bya ba//rnal 'byo(ra) rjes su rnal 'byor ro//mthim pa bsgrub pa gnyis dang ni/de bzhin bsnyen pa gnyis kyang ngo //rnal 'byor thig   
5b1(rgyud): ler bshad pa ste//phra mo rnal 'byor rnal 'byor ma'o//dus kyi phyed dang zla ba yis//rdzogs ldan sum gnyis rtsod dus son//'di'i rdo rje zhal bzhi'o//
5b2(rgyud): dga' ba brgyad gnyis su 'gyur to//cha shas gcig tu rnam ldan pa'i//phyogs kyi dbye bas 'gro ba'o//thu du nyi ma'i rgyu ba yis////
5b3(rgyud): ////de bzhin du ni mkhas pa//bud med kun gyi sgyu ma'i rgya//gnyis med ye shes bla na med//
5b4(rgyud): byang chub sems 'od chen por ni//rnal 'byor chen por nges par 'gro//stong nyid snying rje dbyer med de//dngos rnams nam mkha'i me tog bzhin//sad pa 
5b5(rgyud): na yang nyams pa ste//de nas kun du gnas min pa'o//rmi lam yul med las byung ba/dngos po kun la byung ba ste//dbang po kun las byung ba med//gnyis 
5b6(rgyud): log pa'i stobs kyis ni//sa gsum po ni bza' bar bya//gnyid mthug skyes pa'i dus de ltar //sad med gnyid med rmi lam med//'das pa'i bde ba'i dus 
5b7(rgyud): rnamasu//sangs rgyas nyid kyang lags par gnas//mkha' 'gro kun dngos sems pa ni//rdo rje mkha' 'gro mchog tu bde//las kyi yul ni bstan byas na//
6a1(rgyud): @#/ O /sum bcu rtsa gsum du lhung ngo//ma rig pas slar des par ni//'gro ba dmyal sogs 'jigs (pa)che//de phyir sangs rgyas chos dge 'dun//rdzu 'phrul che 'dir rjes
6a2(rgyud): chags bya//ji ltar bcom ldan 'das bdag po//mkha' 'gro ma kun gyi ni ming //de ni (bdag po)sems dpas gsungs//mkha'agro kun la bdag mchog tu//gsang ba der ni bsdud byas nas//phag mo lha
6a3(rgyud): rdzas rnal 'byor ma//gnyis med tshig gis ji ltar bzheng+sa//de bzhin yang nas yang du dris//mkha'agro ma'i bdag pos dka' stsal pa//phra mo'i rnam pa rtsa gsum ste//rnam par sh+se
6a4(rgyud): pa gsum gyi rgyu//de phyir phra mor rig bya ste//rtsa byung ba ni bdag nyid che//sum bcu rtsa bdun rnal 'byor ma//re re la ni de yongs ldan//mkha'agro ma kun de bshad de/gzhan 
6a5(rgyud): yang de ltar ngo mtshar che//ming mdor bstan pa'i rgya mtsho 'dir//de kun ji lta bzhin bshad bya//zla ba 'o ma bsnam pa mo//'jam ma 'dod pa kun sbyin ma//ma dang sring 
6a6(rgyud): mo bu mo dang//srid mo'i bu mo ni ne mo//gnyen mo pha'i ma dang ni//zhang po yi yang chung ma dang//pad can dung can sna tshogs ma//glang po med dang myos bslabs ma/gzugs 
6a7(rgyud): ma sgra ma dri ma dang /ro ma reg ma sgra ma dri ma dang/ro ma reg ma yid ma dang //chos dbyings ma dang tshogs can ma//gnyid ma le lo gzhan gnon ma//rlon pa me dang sgeg mo
6b1(rgyud): dang//bzang zhal ma dang drag mo ma//'thung zhing za ma 'jigs pa mo//ha ha sgrogs ma du khrod ma//bcom ldan dge sa mar rtag tu bshad//sum bcu rtsa bdun rnal 'byor ma//
6b2(rgyud): las kyi cho ga blangs pa yis//gzhan gyi ming yang shes par bya//gang zhig gang gis mngon brjod pa'i//rtsa de 'dir ni brjod pa'o//de rnams sems dpa' bde rang bzhin//mthar thug
6b3(rgyud): mchog du zhi ba'o//rdo rje mkha' 'gro'i dngos po yang //mchog tu zag med rnal 'byor ldan//mdog dang phyag sogs rnam pa ru//srid pa'i 'khor lo yang dag byung //gnas dang 
6b4(rgyud): nye ba'i gnas sogs 'dir//pu li ra sogs dbus su'o//gang zhig gang gi lha'i bdag po//de ni de dang de rgyas gdab//nyin dang bdag du gtsen gyur pas//bde ba kun
6b5(rgyud): dang yang dag ldan//'di yis rang 'byung bcom ldan 'das//gcig pu de bzhin dang po'i lha//mkha'agro ma kun bde ster ba'i//bcom ldan rdo rjes 'di gsungaso//namakha'
6b6(rgyud): thog ma tha ma med//gang 'drar mchog tu 'du shes pa//de 'drar bdag ni rig pa ste//snang med par yang yongasu gsal//der ni 'byung ba 'ang yod min te//'jig+sa rten 
6b7(rgyud): pa'i tha snyad mchog/ji ltar mkha' las me byung ba//bla na med me 'dir de bzhino//ro bro ba ni gcig dngos la//de'i nang nas dngos mang po//gcig dang du ma'i rang bzhin 'dis/
7a1(rgyud): @#/ O /thams cad ro ste ro khyab bdag / ྾ /sa gsum po ni mchog du za//thams dang shes rab snying rje che//lha'i bdag po gzhan med do//'dod chags ma yin chags bral min//dbu
7a2(rgyud): mar yang ni mi dmigaso//de ltar rtag shes rnal 'byor pas//'dod bya kun la longs spyad bya//rtag tu nang du gyur byas nas//kha dog rnam ni zhal las byung //rtag tu lus kyi sbyin pa ste//sem+sa
7a3(rgyud): las byung ba spangs bar bya//de ltar 'dod chags rgyu mtha' la//rol pa mchog gi go 'phang ngo //'dod chags bral (ba) (ga'i) (ba)gsum(ba)//yangs pa dbu ma zag pa med //gzungs par bya ba 
7a4(rgyud): min pa ste//dngos po'i rang bzhin 'dod chags min//bral ba'i dga' ba nyams pa ste//de la 'ang dga' ba mi bya'o//de gnyis dbus na gnas pa yi//chos de la yang dga' dmigs med//
7a5(rgyud): dbye ba med pa yi ni chos//rdo rje la ni dga' bar gyis//lha'i rnam pa'i rnal 'byor bdag/skad cig mngon gsum byed pa'o//lugs ma la sogs rnal 'byor gyis//rnam par byas pa'i
7a6(rgyud): dbyibasu 'gyur//stong nyid rang bzhin gzugs rnal 'byor//rang byin brlabs pa'i mtshan nyid do//bdag byin brlab dang bral rnal 'byor//sbun lkogs dum bu bzhin shes bya//'di
7a7(rgyud): 'dra'i mtshan nyid mchog gyur pa//skye bo phyugs rnams kyis mi shes//de'i sgyu 'phrul 'di bsgoms na//byang chub sems ni zhes(pa) thob//skye pa 'di la glen pa nyid //shes 
7b1(rgyud): dang snyan ngag skyes bur 'gyur//'di 'dra'i yon tan tshogs ldan pas//rmi lam dang ni mnyam par ltos//rnal 'byor rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud//'gro ste de bzhin 'jig rten 
7b2(rgyud): pa'o//mngon gsum rang nyid sngags kyi rgyud//'khor lo gsum bdag nyis 'gyur ro//gcig la 'khor lo gsum pa ste//gnyis pa nyis 'gyur du ni 'dod//'khor lo gsuM
7b3(rgyud): po thabs shes rab//snyom par 'jug par 'chad pa'o//de ni nyis 'gyur shes bya ste//'khor lo nyis 'gyur nges bshad do//khams gsum gi ni gnas chen po//dkyil 'khor
7b4(rgyud): 'khor lo bsgom pa'o//dpa' bo thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i mchog/de bzhin gshegs pa dpa' dbang che//bcom ldan rdo rjes 'di gsung+sa
7b5(rgyud): so//chos dang chos min gang du nub//byang chub der ni sems bzung gyis//grub pa lha dang lha ma yin//dbang po dang ni yul dang ni//khams dang skye mched tham+sa
7b6(rgyud): cad ni//skye ba dang ni 'gag pa ste//rmongs pa 'ching zhing mkhas pa ni//yongasu shes pas grol ba'o//sems 'dir rang nyid mngon sum du//khams gsum du ni 
7b7(rgyud): rnam par gnas//bdag dang mchog khyod gnyis med las//tshogs pa'i gtso bo byung ba'o//spyod lam la sogs bye brag chos//mdo sde rnam shes dbu ma ba//mos pa'i 
8a1(rgyud): @#/ O /sems can rnams bshad de//bden pa ma yin brdzun pa min//phyag ni bcu gnyis dang ldan te//de bzhin zhal ni bzhi pa'o//kha dog namakha' sngon po la/
8a2(rgyud): spyan gsum brkyang bskum ldan pa ste//'phrog byed dkar po mnyam par mnan//gzhan ni 'bum mtsho stong phrag go/gang dang gang gi rgyud kyi ni//phyi dang gsang ba nang bdag nyid//de 
8a3(rgyud): dang de'i gzugs nges par//lha ni he ru ka'i bdag nyid//rgyud dang rgyud kyi nang mchog tu//dbye ba mos pa'i dbang gi(sa) te//ming ni de 'drar shes par bya//'on kyang he ru ka bsres pa'o//
8a4(rgyud): kha dog de dang 'drar shes pa//bzhugs tshul he ru ka'i rnam pa//sa ni bcu gnyis rnams kyi rgyu//de yis he ru ka dang 'dra//rdo rje mkha'agro de bzhin gshegs//dpa' bo thams cad 
8a5(rgyud): mnyam sbyor ba//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i mchog/bcom ldan dpa' bos 'di gsungaso//dpal mkha'agro rgya mtsho rnal 'byor rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po las/
8a6(rgyud): ye shes (kyi )rgya mtsho la 'jug pa ste//'di ni le'u dang po'o//(!//phag mo lha rdzas rnal 'byor mas//slar yang rdo rje mchod bya ste//bcoM ldan bdag pos bstan par mdzod/
8a7(rgyud): phag mo ci yis yongasu byung//bskyed ba gang gis bskyed pa yis//rang bzhin ji lta bur ni 'dod//bdag byung ba'i rgyu mtshan ci//'dir ni dgos pa ji ltar yin//dbang po ni yul
8b1(rgyud): thams cad//lte ba'i dkyil 'khor du rab zhugs//spyi bo'i rdo rje gur du ni//steng du kha ltas rlung 'bab po//'dod chag+sa nyi ma'i dbusu ni//skad cig gis ni sbrul thim 'gyur//de skyes pa'i 
8b2(rgyud): phag mo ni//b+ha ga ling ka yid las byung //bde ba chen po'i rang bzhin khams//de rnams las ni sngon du skyes//rang bzhin bdag med gcig po ste//mkha' snyam rnal 'byor gzung+sa
8b3(rgyud): cano//rgyu mtshan skye ba sgrol ba dang //snying rje gzug+sa rang bzhin du byung //'di ni dgos pa gnyis rig ste//phyag rgya sbyor bas rab bsgrub pa'o//khyod ni lha mo'i bdag po ste//kun du 
8b4(rgyud): spyod pa mang spyod pa'o//sngags kyi phreng ba brjid byas na//a yig lasogs de'i khyab bdag/yang na phag mo'i rtsa ba dang //go cha'i sngag+sa rnams kyis ldan te//yi ge re re'i sngag+sa kyi(sa) ni/
8b5(rgyud): spyod pa sdom par son pa'o//rnal 'byor ma mchog skye ba ni//rtsa'i mchog gi grong du ste//stong phrag bdun bcu rtsa gnyis gyi//dbus ma a ba d+hu ti'o//dbu ma 'di ltar thams cad kyi/
8b6(rgyud): khyab byed du ni rnam par gnas//de phyir phag mo'i gtso mo yin//go 'phang mchog bzhin shes par bya//rdo rje la sogs ming thog mar//a rdo rje dang ka rdo rje//sngags mthar de lasog+sa
8b7(rgyud): pa ste//yang na so so tha dad du'o//gang gi dusu de bzlos pa//de'i dusu rnal 'byor ldan//lte ba'i spyi bo'i snying gyen du//'di ltar 'gro dang 'ongs ba byed//dpa' bo rnal 'byor 
9a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ma gtso mo//de ni bdag nyid rnam par 'phro//rang gi 'khor ldan rnal 'byor ma//zhing dang zhing rnamasu ni 'gro/lha mo khyod ni bdag po ste//nga dang lhan cig kha sbyor ro//
9a2(rgyud): dus dang dus ni ma yin par//namakha'i bu ga'i nang du gnas//de bzhin skye gnas lam thon nas//'chi ba med pa'i sa bon 'dzag/der ni pho med ye shes bya//hi ka phyed yi ges bsgom
9a3(rgyud): bya ste//mi mo skyes bu'i dusu yang //mchog tu zag med mngon par bya//der byung ba'i rig ma che//bsgom pa'i bdag nyid kyis bri'o//thog mar (rnam )snang mgon po ste//ri mo gsum skor
9a4(rgyud): lte 'og gnas//langs nas 'gri ba'i bar du 'gro//sa gsum sgron ma lta bur son//gzhan pa'i sngags ni sa mtha' 'dra//de las zla 'bab g.yon naso//sngag(sa) 'di thig les brgyan pa ste/
9a5(rgyud): sngag(sa) ni rnal 'byor mas skyed pa'o//slar yang sa bon gnyis pa brjod//slar yang sa bon gnyis pa brjod//g.yas pa nyi ma bskyed pa ste//logasu gnyis phye 'gor thig le//yang de'i mtha'i
9a6(rgyud): sa bon ni//byang chub sems chen lam 'bab mo//'di ni phag mo'i sa bon yin//a'i yi ge mthar brgyan pa//bcu drug zla   phyed nyi mas brgyan//ya sde'i dang po'i sa bon ni//sbyor ba(sa)
9a7(rgyud): sa gsum rlung yon tan//dbyang(sa) kyi bcu gsum pas rgyas gdab//dkar po'i phyogs kyi mgo bor brgyan//thig le dang ni bcas par 'bar//dga' ba bzhi po dag pa'o//pa sde'i mtha'
9b1(rgyud): /ma bde ba'i gzugs//a'i yi ge zhon ldan pa//mgo bor zla phyed nyi mas mnan//sa'i pha rol gzhon med pa//nyi ma yi ni gdan la gnas//dbyangs kyi bzhi ba rna drung bzungs//mgo
9b2(rgyud): bor zla phyed thig les brgyan//'dod chags mtha'i rang bzhin no//ye shes sa bon nyis 'gyur dbye//spro ba dang ni bsdu bas skyes//'og dang steng du gnas pa yis//mgo bo long spyod
9b3(rgyud): rdzogs 'dzin pa//snying kha dang ni chu skyes su'o//don gyi cho ga yongs rdzogs pa'o//yongasu skrag byed ma sa bon//pa mtha'i yi ge bzung nas ni//slar yang de bzhin brjod par bya//
9b4(rgyud): dbu brgyan 'bar ba 'dzin grong gi/bu gar gtum mo sbor ba'o//byang chub sems kyis sa gsum 'grengs//de ltar bshad pa'i sngags gang ni//rim pas yi ge rnams mi mthun//
9b5(rgyud): rnal 'byor ma'i ming ldan par//las dus de ltar rtogs par bya//re re'i 'che ba'i bdag nyid ni//yi ge yi ni drangs kyi so//bdag nyid che bo rnam par 'phro//byang chub cha shas chu rgyu'o//
9b6(rgyud): thog mar rnam snang phyag 'tshal ba//chos kyi dbyings ni bya ba'i gzugs//ta sde lnga pa'i yi ge ni//lnga pa yi ni sde tshan no//rnal 'byor ma gzugs namakha' rten//pa mtha'i sa bon a dbyang
9b7(rgyud): ldan//gti mug 'joms par byed ma ste//rnal 'byor ma'i tshogs spro'o//de'i bzhi pa'i sa bon ni//sa gsum du ni khyab pa yi//rnal 'byor ma ni spro ba'o//thog ma'i sde'i gsum pa ste//mkha' gro
10a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ma'i 'khor lo spro//ya sde'i mtha' ma'i sa bon ni//byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin no//lnga pa'i sde'ithog ma ste/de bzhin nyid kyi sa bon ste//i'i dbyangs kyis 
10a2(rgyud): mgo la reg/chu'i yi ge yongs bsags pa//thams cad byed pa'i rang bzhin no//tsa sde'i dbus nas bzung nas ni//nyi ma yi ni bdan ldan pa//las kyi dus su khyad par 'bar//phag
10a3(rgyud): mo yi ni sa bon ni//a'i yi ge rnam mar reg/sa gsum ye shes rang bzhin mnyam//me'i sa bon dbyangs gnyis ldan//'khor ba'i las kyi rang bzhino//sa mtha'i mgo la I
10a4(rgyud): dbyangs reg/rtag tu tshangs pa'i rang bzhin blta//dbyangs kyi gnyis pa bzungs nas nas ni//de ltar mkha'agro ma'i gzugs//sems can sgrol ba'i byed pa'o//ya yig 
10a5(rgyud): gnas par dang ldan//slar yang a'i dbyangs sbyar ro//khams gsum rnal 'byor ma spro'o//drug pa'i sde'i dang po ni//rang gnas pa ni a dbyangs ldan//sdang ba'i sem+sa
10a6(rgyud): can gsong byed 'gyur//dza'i yi ge yongs bzung ste/i'i dbyangs dang yang dag ldan//sgyu ma rang bzhin yongasu ldan pa'o//so'i sa bon rna ba la//e'i dbyang+sa kyis reg
10a7(rgyud): par bya//klu'i sngags ni sbyar bya ste//tre'i yi ge so gsum kun//ye shes kyi ni khyad par sngag+sa/me'i yige o dang ldan//'jig rten rnams kyi rten yin te//byang chub byed pa'i chos
10b1(rgyud): yin no//thog ma'i ka ni ya stan ldan//'chi dang lhan skyes dbye ba med//pa yi sde yi tha ma la//a'i yi ges dbyangs sbyar bya//sa gsum gyi ni ma'i rang bzhin//ta yig a'i yi g+si
10b2(rgyud): rgyan//bde ba 'dzin par byed pa'o//slar yang pa bsde'i mtha' ma ste//tshad med pa'i lhan skyes kyi//dbus na bzhugs pa'i rang bzhin no//sa'i mtha' ma a dang ldan//
10b3(rgyud): rdor sems ye shes gzhom med pa'o//ya sde'i mtha' ma i dbyangs ldan//mi shes rang bzhin 'joms byed pa'o//te sngags chos dbyings rang bzhin te/thams cad bzhin
10b4(rgyud): du khyab pa'o//bu ga rnams nas 'dzag pas//'bab pa'i sa bon nyi ma ldan//chu srin dbang po yi ni sngags//pa sde bzhi pa'i sa bon ni//u dbyangs stan dang ldan pa ste/
10b5(rgyud): 'khor ba'i 'jigs pa nyams byed pa'o//ta'i sngags kyis dgra nyams byed//slar yang sdi'i bzhi pa ni//sgra chen po ni sgrogs pa'o//rlung gi sa bon a yig reg/sngags ni
10b6(rgyud): nor bu 'bab bzhin pa'o//chu'i sa bon rnar bzung ba//sgyu ma yi ni rang bzhin no//sa'i pha rol e yig ldan//sa gsum gyi ni 'khrul 'joms rgyu//pa sde'i mtha' ma
10b7(rgyud): rkang rang bzhin//sa'i pha rol a dang ldan//ngo mtshar che ba yi ni chos//ya sde'i yi ni mtha' ma ni//pad+ma'i sa bon mchog yig go/rtsa'i dbus ma'i sa bon no//ra'i mgo
11a1(rgyud): @#/ O /la gnas pa ste//e yig gis ni phye ba yin//lha dang lha min rang bzhino//de ltar sngags tshig drug po ste//rnal 'byor ma'i tshogs spro ba//las kun rang bzhin 
11a2(rgyud): 'khor lo'o//snying rje'i 'dod par gyur pa yi//de kho na nyid tshig rgya che//rnal 'byor thams cad rab 'gengs pa'i //bcom ldan mkha'agros 'di gsungaso//'khrul 'khor 'khor
11a3(rgyud): lo bshad bya ste//khyod kyi 'khrul 'khor chung rgya che//kun nas yongasu zlum po ni//ri mo bcu sum bri bar bya//lha mo dum bu bcu gnyis 'gyur//sum bcu rtsa drug dbyibs kyi(sa)
11a4(rgyud): brgyan//rtsibs re re la yi ge ni//rims pas yongasu gsal bar sbyin//lhag ma nyi shu bzhi yi ge/thams cad lte ba'i snying po'o//de rnams kyis ni bskyed pa'i rtsa//stong 
11a5(rgyud): phrag bdun bcu rtsa gnyiso//so sor re re yi ni 'khor//drang srong mda' dang zla ba'o//kun nas lhag pas dman pa ni//nor dang rig byed tshig(sa) pa'o//der gnas pa la
11a6(rgyud): 'khrul 'khor du//las cig ji ltar mos pa'o//rnam par shes pa'i sbyor ba ni//rtsa ba thams cad 'char dusu//der ni dbang po dang ni yul//las kyi dusu bsad par bya/
11a7(rgyud): yid ces bya ba bskrang bya zhing //sems ni dbang du bya ba ste//khams dang skye mched gzhan de bzhin//zhi ba dang ni rgyas par bya//phung po kun dang de bzhin srog/dgug pa dang ni sdang
11b1(rgyud): ba'o//lus dang ngag dang de bzhin sems//de rnams nges par bya ba'o//las gzhan ji ltar rigs pa yis//brgyad kyis lhag pa'i brgyad yang ngo//yang nas bon ci 'dra ba//de'i
11b2(rgyud): tshad dang mnyam las so//de dus bdag nyid che ba yis//yi ges brgyad dang 'joms pa'o//phag po rdo rje gtso mo yi//'khrul gnyis pa bshad bya ste//ya sde'i mtha'i sa
11b3(rgyud): bon rang bzhin no//lce gnyis yi ge ra yig ldan//rnal 'byor ma sngags a yig dgang //sa gnas pa ni mi g.yo ba'o//na'i yi ge e dang ldan//sems can kun sprul
11b4(rgyud): rgyu mtshan no//thog nas dbyangs ni thog ma'i mgon//lce gnyis i dbyangs mgo la reg/sa gsum khams kyi rang bzhin no//ta'i yi ge a dang ldan//lnga pa yi ni thog ma
11b5(rgyud): ste//rnal 'byor ma'i rang bzhin no//ya sde'i bzhir gnas pa'i sa bon//kun gyi gtso mo'i rang bzhin no//brgyad pa'i sde pa'i thog sa bon//stong pa yis ni brgyan pa ste/khu
11b6(rgyud): ba'i khams kyi ngo bo nyid//gnyis par gnas pa'i thog mar ni//keng rus thig le 'dzin pa'o//ya sde'i gnyis pa'i sa bon ni//e'i dbyangs dang ldan pa ste//rdo rje snying
11b7(rgyud): gnas mgon po'o//ta tha a'i yi ge brgyan//skye gnas la ni smras pa'o//ta yig nyi ma dang ldan pa//rims 'joms pa'i rgyal po'o//b+ha ni nyi ldan a yig brgyan//
12a1(rgyud): @#/ O /lte ba'i thab tu gnas pa'o//pa sde'i tha ma gnas rang bzhin//Ta mtha' e yis brgyan pa ni//'gugs pa'i yi ni rang bzhin no//bi sha Sha /
12a2(rgyud): Na sngags bzhi ste//dga' ba bzhi yi rang bzhin gyis//gter chen po'i rang bzhin no//ra yig a'i yi ges brgyan//tsA drag po'i rang bzhino//sha'i gnyis pa'i sa bon ni//
12a3(rgyud): snying po yi ni gzugs su'o//Na yig rang bzhin gyis gzungs pa//rnal 'byor ma'i nga nyid de ngo bo//ka yig ra yig o yig ldan//dbus ma brkyang ma ro rang bzhin//ta ba'i
12a4(rgyud): bar nas bzung bar bya//rnal 'byor ma'i tshogs ki ni//rkang pa brgyad pa bsgrub rang bzhin//ta sde'i tha ma a yig brgyan//thod pa'i dbus kyi rtsa rang bzhin//ka ru mngon 
12a5(rgyud): brjod bzung bar bya//de yis las rnams zad par byed//ra yig a yi yi ges bzung //mgo bo'i dbus nas sgra chen 'dzag/sa'i sa bon a yig ldan/gru
12a6(rgyud): gsum gyi ni dbusu'o//ta'i mtha'am is brgyan pa//sprul pa'i steng dang 'og du'o//ta yig ra yig a yig sbyar//chos kyi dbyings ni gru gsuM ste//de phyir sa
12a7(rgyud): gsum bskyed pa'o//sa bzung dbus a bcas sa bon//na yig a ding ldan rang bzhin//pa sde'i mtha' ma a yis brgyan//sgyu ma'i ngo bo pad+ma'i rtsa//rang bzhin 
12b1(rgyud): gnas pa rna rang bzhin//Ta sde'i mtha' ma i dang ldan //ma lus dbyer med sems can gyi//khams 'dzin pa'i mgon po'o//de ltar sngags tshig bcu brjod de/
12b2(rgyud): dus bzhi yi ni tshad can no//rnal 'byor ma kun las kyi ni//'khor lo bsgyur ba longs spyod la//dus kyi gzugs kyi rang bzhin can//bcom ldan lha yis 'di 
12b3(rgyud): gsungaso//mkha'agro ma gnas 'du ba ni//'khor lo 'khor lor shes pa'o//nyi khri brgya phrag bcu drug gi/ye shes las ni de bzhin byed //yon por smra 
12b4(rgyud): ba rnams kyi ni//de rdul grangs las skyes par 'dod //bsgrub par bya ba'i ming              bzung nas //phyi nas blo ldan gyis sngags sbyar //sngon du pa la rab stan no//
12b5(rgyud): thog ma yi ni chos gzugaso//de yi bzhi par gnas pa la//e'i yi ges rnar brgyan pa//rnal 'byor ma rnams kyi sngagaso//ta sde'i dbus gnas ci 'dod ster//
12b6(rgyud): na yig e'i yi ger ldan//sna tshogs sprul ba byed ba'o//pa yig spros pa dang bcas pa'o///ra'i yi ge a dang ldan//rnal 'byor ma'i 'khor lo bsgyur//
12b7(rgyud): ca sde'i dbus gnas sa bon bzung //gang la de ltar rang bzhin no//rlung bsags pa ni e yig ldan//a haM yi ge'i rang bzhin no//dbyangs kyi gsum pas mgo/
13a1(rgyud): @#/ O /dbyangs kyi gsum pas mgo la brgyan//sna tshogs ye shes sngags rang bzhin //dza'i sa bon mtha' med par//'bar bar 'gyur ba yi ni gzugs//yi ge ya ni 
13a2(rgyud): e yig ldan//theg pa kun gyi blta ba'i gzugs//tsa sde'i gsum pa'i sa bon no//rnal 'byor ma'i gzugs zhes bya//pa mtha' de'i bzhi pa'o//sngags thams cad kyi bla ma'o//
13a3(rgyud): ta sde'i mtha' ma dbyangs gsum ldan//ma lus sems can bde ba'i gzugs//'dra ba'i gsum pa ta stan can//'gro dang mi 'gro bcas 'gro sgrol //de bzhin ma b+ha'i yi ge
13a4(rgyud): bzung//sa ni bcu bzhi pa'i gzugs//ni sngags rnal 'byor ma rnams kyi//brnga yis su ni 'jug pa'o//pa sde'i mtha' ma o yig reg//sna tshogs mi shes 'dzin pa'o/
13a5(rgyud): sa mtha' rdor sems rang bzhin no//di'i yi ge chos dang ni//chos min sun 'byin pa yino//ya yi sde mtha' ma ni/rnal 'byor ma sta namakha'i gzugs//dza yig
13a6(rgyud): ra yig stan can no//khu ba'i rang bzhin shar ba'o//slar yang rlung gi mtha' ma ni//a yi yi ge(sa) gzi(sa)ga skyed pa//ra yig a yi yi ge ldan//'gyur ba med pa'i
13a7(rgyud): rang bzhin no//sa mtha' dbyangs gsum mgo la gnas//rnal 'byor ma rnams 'bab pa'i gzugs//ma ni dbus ma de nyid gzugs//sa mtha' a yis bkang pa ste/
13b1(rgyud): rnal 'byor ma gzugs kyi khar brjod//ya yig o'i gcod pan gnas//lhan cig skyes pa'i rang bzhino//ka sde'i dbus gnas dbyangs gsum ldan//de nyid med pa'i 
13b2(rgyud): rang bzhino//na'i yi ge rang bzhin med//gnyis pa yi ni thog ma la//a'i yi ge gsal bar byas//'dod pa'i khams kyi rang bzhin ni/pa sde'i mtha' ma e dang ldan//thams 
13b3(rgyud): cad sgyu ma'i rang bzhin no//la yi sa bon mthar sha ste//ya sde'i mtha' yig stan can ni//khu ba dang ni rlung dbyer med//i dbyangs mgor brgyan me sa bon//grub pa sgyu ma'i
13b4(rgyud): rang bzhin no//ka'i gnyis pa'i sa bon ni//namakha' rnal 'byor ma'i gzugs//sa bon de'i mtha' yi ge/e yi yi ge rnam brgyan pa//dpa' bo gcig pu mi bskyed pa'o/
13b5(rgyud): de ltar 'khor lo mkha' rang bzhin//de ltar rkang pa dgu pa ni//rnal 'byor ma tshogs yi ge ste//khams gsum gyi ni sngags rang bzhin//sngags kun spyod pa'i sbyor bdag 
13b6(rgyud): nyid//mkha'agro'i dam tshig la bzhugs nas//dngos grub thams cad rab gsol ba'i//bcom ldan lha yis 'di gsungaso//'khor lo gsum pa'i nus pa yis//
13b7(rgyud): ji ltar 'dod pa'i las bya'o//rnal 'byor rnams kyi las dus su//sgyu mas thams cad rmongs par bya//rdo rje ma bdag slar langs nas//'khor lo gzhan gyi rnal 'byor 
14a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ldan//mchog du mi 'gyur tso bo la//bdag med ma yis dam mkhyud de//ta yi sngags kyis gnyis pa sbyor//rgya gnas me dang rlung sa bon//bde ba chen po'i 
14a2(rgyud): 'dod chags che//de yis gnyis pa'i sa bon ni//a'i yi ges dgang pa'o//'byung ba chen po'i gzhi rang bzhin//pa yi( ) ra yig o yig sbyar//gsum po rlung gi rang bzhin no//
14a3(rgyud): ta'i yi ge a yig ldan //slar yang rnam shes sa bon no//ka'i sde'i dbus gnas pa//e yi yi ges rnam par brgyan//rnal 'byor ma phyag rgya rang bzhin//
14a4(rgyud): slar la yang ni brjod par bya'o//tshig 'di thabs shes dbyer med dngos//sa (ma)tha' dang ni na yi sngags//slar la yang ni brjod par ( )bya'o//bskyed cing bsdud par byed
14a5(rgyud): gzugaso//slar yang a yig sa bon cig/don dam pa'i yi ge'o//Ta mtha' a yi yi ge ldan//gzhon du med las skyes pa'o//ta'i mtha' ma kun 'joms pa'o/
14a6(rgyud): ka ka dang ni med gnas la//dbyangs gsum sbyar zhing thig le sbyin//rnal 'byor ma kun 'bod pa'o//de bzhin du ni kha kha dang //gsum pa Na ra gnas pa'o//dub pa sel
14a7(rgyud): bar byed pa'i rgyud//d+hu na yi ni sngags kyi tshigs//lan gnyisu ni brjod par bya//rtog pa thams cad 'jom(sa) par byed//ya sde'i mtha' ma yi ni sngags//rdo rje'i 
14b1(rgyud): bu gar brgyan pa'o//tsa sde'i dbus gnas sa bon ni//ra'i stan la yongasu gnas gti mug za bar byed pa'o//sa mtha' gzhom med dbus ma'o//shi sde'i gsum pa ha 
14b2(rgyud): stan can //a yi yi ges rna bar brgyan//sna tshogs mkha'agro ma'i gzugs//'bod par byed pa yi ni sngags//sho Sha ya'i sngags kyi tshig/lan gnyisu ni brjod par bya//ye
14b3(rgyud): shes can rlom nga rgyal 'joms//byang chub byang chub med de nyid//'di ltar rkang pa bcu gnyis ni//sa dang rnal 'byor las rang bzhin//bcu gnyis sa rnams khyab bdag
14b4(rgyud): gi/bcom ldan lha yis 'di gsungaso//yi ge rnams kyi gnas sogs kyi//brda' yis de nyid pha rol 'gro/bdag las sems can thams cad skye//sems can las
14b5(rgyud): kyang bdag 'byung ngo //bdag de rtag tu med gyur nas//sgyu ma rmi lam de bzhin gshegs//'khrul 'khor 'di ni stobs che ste//las rnams ji lta bar ni 'byung //rdo rje mkha'
14b6(rgyud): 'gro'i mtshan nyid du//dus 'di thams cad 'gyur ba'o//'khor lo lnga pa 'di rnams su//mya ngan 'das pa 'ang brjod par bya//lha sogs thams cad rgyun du gsod//de
14b7(rgyud): rnams thams cad rtag tu grol//bka' stsal pa//ka'i gnyis pa'i sa bon ni//rnal 'byor ma tshogs de nyid do//Ta bzung pa mtha'i sten can te//a gi gcod pan rgyan par bya//
15a1(rgyud): @#/ O /de la a'i yi ges dgang//'gro ba drug gi rang bzhin no//ka sde'i dbus gnas sa bon ni//dug gis rig pa 'phrog par byed//ka'i sa bon steng rang bzhin/
15a2(rgyud): na'i mtha' ma a yig ldan //me 'bar ba'i dpal ldan te//'dzin pa yi ni sa bon ni//bskyod pa'i sa bdag chen po'o//ta'i sde'i bzhi pa ni//a'i dbyangs kyis brgyan
15a3(rgyud): pa'o//srid pa dang bral ba gzugaso//nyi ma i yig ldan ro langs//Ta mtha'i sa bon bzung bar bya//dbyangs kyi gsum pa mgo la brgyan//sa gsum thams cad gsod
15a4(rgyud): pa'o//pa sde'i mtha' ma'i sa bon ni//yid kyis smra ba rtog med byed//sa mtha' a'i ngag ldan pa//thar pa'i ye shes ni thog ma'o//ma gnyis ha gnyis brjod par bya//rmong+sa
15a5(rgyud): pa'i rang bzhin 'phrog cing 'phrog//pa'i sa bon i yig brgyan//phag nags bzung nyams kun de nyid//sha'i yi ge e dang ldan//seng ge za ba yi ni sngags//ta sngag+sa
15a6(rgyud): slar yang 'chi med skye//sa mtha' a'i yi ges dgangs//thig le cod pan la reg pa'o//rgya la 'chi ba rtog pa yin//sa yig dbyangs gnyis brjod phyir rgyan//sngon du
15a7(rgyud): 'phrog par byed pa'o//sha gnas sa bon dur khrod ste//ta mtha' i dang ldan pa ni//thams cad rang bzhin med pa'o//pa sde'i mtha' ma i dbyangs dga'//sdong ba'i yid ni 'dzin
15b1(rgyud): chen po//na yig u stan rtog kun sel//pa sde'i gnyis pa'i sa bon ni//a yis mgo reg thig le gnas//byang chub bsgrub pa (yi)ni sngags//thur du sel ba'i rlung 
15b2(rgyud): gis so//ta dang ra'i yi ge gnyis//sa ni 'jigs par byed pa'i sngags//pa ra a'i sa bon cig (ga)suM bcu rtsa gsum skyob dub 'joms//ya sde'i mtha' mar ri mnan pa//ye she(sa)
15b3(rgyud): kyi ni dag byed do//Na'i 'gram gnas sa bon ni//a yis rna bar rgyan pa'o//rnal 'byor pa yi mi shes 'joms//sa yig e'i yiges dgang //dbugs 'chad pa ni nyam(sa) par 
15b4(rgyud): byed//na sngags dbyangs gsum mgo la brgyan//chos dang chos min yongs rang bzhin//d+ha yig ya dang sbyar ba ste//bsam gtan sngags kyi rang bzhin no//ta sde'i mtha' ma 
15b5(rgyud): bzung bya ste//grong khyer ltar snang dngos(pa) med //ya sde'i gnyis pa'i sa bon no//gti mug rang bzhin 'bar ba'o//seng ge ma he 'joms pa'o//nyi mar rang ni gnas pa ni//rnal 'byor
15b6(rgyud): ma kun rgyal po'o//pa sde'i mtha' ma a dbyangs brgyan//sgyu mar sems can kun nges 'dzin//'dzin pa'i sa bon a yig brgyan//de kho na'i ye shes thob//de ltar rkang pa bcu bzhi'o//
15b7(rgyud): sems can kun gyi mi shes gsod //dam pa'i sngags dngos gzugs bdag nyid//'byin cing bsdud par byed pa'o//'byung po thams cad 'jigs byed pa//bcom ldan lha yis 
16a1(rgyud): @#/ O /'di gsungaso//'khor lo'i rnam pa'i gzugs bdag nyid//phag mo'i thugs khar brgyan pa ste//stong nyid snying rje dbyer med pas//gsod pa dang ni bsod pa'o//
16a2(rgyud): ka sde'i dbus gnas ra stan can//gsum de'i mgo la thig le sbyin//theg pa mchog gi chos can no//ta sde'i gnyis pa'i yi ge ni//e'i dbyangs dang ldan pa'o//mu steg+sa rnal 
16a3(rgyud): 'byor nyams byed pa'o//ta yig rtogs par byed pa'o//ta sde'i gzhir gnas a dang ldan//byang chub mthar med 'dzin pa'o//reg nas pa la dbyangs gsum brgyan//rnal 'byor
16a4(rgyud): ma kun 'char bar ro//na sngags a yig gis brgyan pa'o//rnal 'byor ma rnams kyi lam mo//sum b+ha ni yi tshig rkang ste//lan gnyisu ni brjod par bya//tshig drug rtogs pa'i
16a5(rgyud): de nyid do//de bzhin du ni sum b+ha'i sngags//rnal 'byor ma'i mchod rang bzhin//ha na'i sngags ni lan gnyis brjod//sdang ba'i theg pa nyams par byed //pa ra a'i yi ge gcig
16a6(rgyud): dbang por rang rang ngo bo gzhan//Na a aM gi yi ges dgang //sa bon 'di ni yon tan sngags//sa yig tshag kun 'dzag pa'o//pa yig ra yi yig brgyan ri//pa gnas namakha'
16a7(rgyud): la 'jug pa'o//sa'i yig gnas kun gyi gzugs//ya sde'i mtha' pa a yis dgang //rlung ni srog gi ye shes so//na yig A'i yi ges dgang //byang chub nang du 'ang mi
16b1(rgyud): /rtog pa'o//thig le mgo la blta bar bya//pa sde'i mtha' ma sbrang chen gzugs//sa pa tha' a yis dgang bar bya//rnal 'byor ma'i tshogs sreg go/pa mtha' a dang ldan pa la//
16b2(rgyud): aM gis cod phan la brgyan pa'o//chos kun gyi ni sprin rang bzhin//sa yig dam chos mi shes gsod//ts+tshe sngags byang chub sems dpa'o//song ba 'khor bas skye ba med/
16b3(rgyud): ta sde'i dbus gnas sa bon ni//da ni don med pa snang ba'o//na la i dang ldan pa ste/'jig rten ma lus 'ching ba nyid//kro sngags gnon pas 'khor ba gcod//d+ha'i 
16b4(rgyud): sa bon 'gro kun 'joms//spros ba med par byed pa'o//mu yi yi ge'i sa bon ni//rmongs pa'i 'jig rten kun gyi las//de ltar phyed bcas tshig bcu bzhi//rtsa
16b5(rgyud): yi 'khor lo yi ni mtha'o//bum pa can la sogs pa'i las//rnal 'byor gang byed gang 'dod byed//'khor lo drug gis rab sbyor bas//ci 'dod las gyi rang
16b6(rgyud): bzhin te//bdag po sbyor ba grub nus can//bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di gsungaso//gtsug tor la sogs skye gnas mthar//lus ngag sems shes nam
16b7(rgyud): mkha' las//lugs 'byung lugs las bzlog pa yis//de nyid byang chub rnam shes bya//'dir ni thabs kyi cha dang ni//shes rab kyi yang cha ru brjod//yang na yi ge re re
17a1(rgyud): @#/ O /yi/bar bar du ste slar yang ni//d+hi sngags lhan skyes rang bzhin te//'khor lo yi ni khyad par gyi//nye ba'i las rnams byed pa'o//daMSh+Ta ka rA li'i 
17a2(rgyud): sngags//'gro ba rnams kyi las rang bzhin//ma sngags ha lnga mu dri'i sngags//rna ba yi ni rang bzhin no//shri he ru ka de ba s+yA gra mahA Shi'i sngags tshig sna'i rang bzhin no//sa ha sa 
17a3(rgyud): shi re re'i sngags//dbyangs kyi bdag po yi ni gzugs//sa ha sra bA ha be'i sngags//sngags kyi yi ge lus kyi gzugs//sha ta sa'i sngags 'khor lo cig/mkha' 'gro ma sngag+sa
17a4(rgyud): 'chi ba dang//skye ba yi ni rang bzhin no//gnyis med rnam pa'i chos las ni//gnyis su sems can 'dzin pa ste//shes rab la ni bde ster ba//bcom ldan bla mas 'di gsung+sa
17a5(rgyud): so//nyan thos la sogs mu stegs rnams//mi shes pa'i phyugs thams cad//de rnams ye shes thob bya'i phyir//tshul lugs 'di ni bstan pa'o//ha sra na ne yi ni sngag+sa//
17a6(rgyud): yul gyi gzugs la reg pa'o//dz+wa li ta te dza se'i sngags//sngags kyi tshig ni yid kyi yul//dz+wa la mu khi'i tshig lus kyi//ping ga la lo tsa na'i sngags////mig gi rnam par shes
17a7(rgyud): pa'o//badzra sha ri rI'i sngags tshig/lus kyi rnam par shes pa'o//badzra sa ne yi ne sngags//sna'i yul la reg pa'o//mi li ta'i sngags kyi tshig/sna'i rnam par shes pa'o//
17b1(rgyud): tsi li te'i sngags kyi tshig/dbyangs kyi bdag po yi ni yul//he sngags gnyis brjod sgra'i yul//sgra dang dbyangs gnyis gyi rnam shes//de ltar sngags tshig byed bcas 
17b2(rgyud): bdun/'chi dang skye ba kun gyi gzugs//phag mo la ni bde dga' ba'i//bcom ldan bdag pos 'di gsungs te//bo la ka k+ko la sbyor bdag nyid//yul dang dbang po
17b3(rgyud): sogs bsod pa'o//sems can gang dang gang rnams kyi//yul gyi chu skyes las skyes te//sems can de dang de rnams kyi//rang rang las rnams kyis gsod pa'o/
17b4(rgyud): yul la sogs pa 'joms pas na//shes rab 'khor lor de bshad do//rgyud dang rgyud rnams kyi ni las//bsgrub po'i snying po'i ye sheso//sa mtha' drug pa'i 
17b5(rgyud): dbyangs stan can//zla dus thig les mgo la brgyan//lan gnyis su ni brjod par bya//rdo rje sems dpa' gsang ba'i gzugs//kha gnyis ng+hu gnyis mu ru gnyis/
17b6(rgyud): las rnams kyi ni dngos po ste//srog dang mnyam du gnas pa'o//a dwA+e'i te mahA yo gi ni//pa thi ta sid+d+ha'i sngags kyi tshig/de yang lus mtha' yi ni gzugs//tre
17b7(rgyud): d+haM tshig gnyis grangs tshig gnyis//he'i tshig gnyis ha tshig gnyis//khro bcu'i yi ge bshin rje ni//mthar byed la sogs kyis gor bya//pa sde'i bzhi pa'i sa bon 
18a1(rgyud): @#/ O /i yis mgo la reg pa'o//me sa'i sngags ni tshig kun te//mkha'agro ma'i rang bzhin no//de ltar sngags tshig bcu gnyis ni//rten 'brel bcu 
18a2(rgyud): gnyis bslu ba ste//ston pa dam tshig chen po gtso//bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di gsungaso//rten 'brel yan lag bcu gnyis kyi//las kyis lhan gcig rmug+sa
18a3(rgyud): byed pa'o//pho nya rnams skyed pa'i yige/'khor lo'i rnam pa'i rang bzhin can//'khor lo yi ge min de ltar //pho nya'i rang bzhin med pa'o//rnam smin de las 
18a4(rgyud): skyes pa yang //so so'i ming du 'du shes te//'jam rdor la sogs dngos po yis//a bad+hu ti'i mtshan nyid bya//ha sa ha sa dpa' bo cig sngags tshig/
18a5(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs drug sa bon te//yon tan mtha' med 'chad pa'o//ha 'i sa bon yi ge bzhi//ho gcig hUM gnyis brjod par bya//yi ge bdun po spag+sa
18a6(rgyud): sogs khams//tra'i lo kya bin shi' sngags//sdang ba la ni go cha'o//sha ta sa ha pra ko Ti/ta thA ga ta pa ri pa re//sa mtha' u yi dbyangs kyis smras//
18a7(rgyud): zla ba'i dum bu thig les brgyan//de ltar sngags kun nyer bsdud pa'o//ha u aM sngags kyi rang bzhin//de ltar sngags kyi tshig ni bzhi//rnal 'byor ma skyed
18b1(rgyud): dngos grub 'gyur//bka' snang dngos grub rab 'jug pa//bcom ldan dpa' bos 'di gsungaso//mkha'agro sdom pa mngon brjod dang//gzhan yang rnal 
18b2(rgyud): 'byor ma'i rgyud dang//rnal 'byor rgyud rnamasu gsungs pa'i//las kyi tshogs dang dngos grub ster//bkas gnang 'khor lo'i rab sbyor bas//'grub 'gyur
18b3(rgyud): sbyor ba gzhan gyis min//slar yang sa mtha' dbyangs lnga pa'i//drug na gnas pa'i stan can la//zla bum thig les brgyan pa'o//cho gas de kho na'i sa bon//ba
18b4(rgyud): dang gnas pa'i rnal 'byor ma'i//tshogs ni skyed par byed pa'o//Da yig 'bar bar byed pa'i sngags//sing ha ru seng tha sngags tshig/bgegs kyi gtso bo rlag byed
18b5(rgyud): pa'o//ga dza tu pe ga'i sngags//sems can kun gyi bgegs byed pa'o//trai lo kyo da re'i tshig/rab mchog pho nya kun skyed pa'o//sa mu tra kha la gra ha gnyis kyi tshig/
18b6(rgyud): rgya mtsho lta bu'i ske rags smras//de ni sngags kyi ske ragaso//hUM dang phaT kyi yi ge ni//mkha'agro ma'i khyab bdag go//bI rA dwe te hUM sngags tshig/thabs dang shes rab 
18b7(rgyud): gnyis med las//tha mal 'dus byas skyes pa'o//de ltar rkang pa bdun sngags te//ye shes pa'i rang bzhin gyis//bcom ldan rdo rjes 'di gsungs te//sems 
19a1(rgyud): @#/ O /can las la grub pa'i mtha'//de'i go 'phang mchog du 'gro//sangs rgyas rdo rje 'chang ba'i ming //slar yang skyes zhing lam la gnas//sgyu ma'i rnam pa 
19a2(rgyud): sgyu ma min//rang gi bdag nyid so sor rig//hUM ha gnyis mahA ba shu mo ha na'i//tshig 'di 'khor ba'i rmongs pa'o//yo ga shwa re tAM gi sngags//zhi ba yi ni gzugasu'o//
19a3(rgyud): DA ki ni lo kA nAM b+han d+ha na'i tshig//'jig rten byang chub tu 'jig byed//sa t+ya me hUM phaT sngags kyi tshig//mtha' bzhi po ni ston pa'o//b+hu ta tra sa na'i tshig//mu stegs ye shes 
19a4(rgyud): 'joms pa'o//de ltar sngags tshig bdun p+so ni//lus kyi dbye ba byed pa ste//pad zla'i 'dab ma re re la//sngags kyi yi ge rnams gnas pa'o//dpa' bo sngags kyi
19a5(rgyud): gzugs gnas pa'i//bcom ldan 'das kyis 'di gsungaso//'khrul 'khor chen po bcu gnyis te//bdag gi 'dza' bo yi ni gsungs//las thams cad kyi 
19a6(rgyud): 'khrul 'khor ni//bcu gnyis rnams kyi lha cig bya//'khor lo chen po re re ru//nges par so so'i las bya'o//phrad pa rnams ni bcu gnyisu//bsgrub bya'i ming ni bsgrub
19a7(rgyud): par bya//pa mtha' sa mtha' a dang ldan//ya mtha'i i'i dbyangs dang bcas//re yig na mtha' rab yig dang//pa mtha' dang ni se d+he'i tshig//bid r+ye'i 
19b1(rgyud): tshig dang shwa ri'i sngags//pa'i drung ma dang DA sa bon//hUM la zla dum thig les brgyan//skyar la yang ni brjod par bya//sa'i drung gnas dang Ta yig ste//hUM phaT dang 
19b2(rgyud): ni swAhA bcas//sngags 'di lte bar cho sa ga yis//bsgrub bya'i ming dang bcas par brjod//bcu gnyis rnams dang lte bca' pa//de bzhin gshegs pas 'di 
19b3(rgyud): gsungaso//sa bon yi ge nyi shu bzhi//bu li ra sogs bsres pa'o//dpal he ru ka rdor phag mo//ma byung 'byung bar mi 'gyuro//sngags 'di yi ni mthu stobs ni/bye ba
19b4(rgyud): stong phrag gsum pa yi//mtha' med gzhung las dpa' bo yis//de bzhin snying po bsdus pa yin//rgyud gzhan du ni gsungs pa'i sngags//sngags 'di yis ni 
19b5(rgyud): bsgrub thabaso//bzlas med grangs med yi ge med//gzugs med bsam gtan de bzhin med//lha dran pa'i ye shes kyis//dngos grub thams cad skyed pa'o//
19b6(rgyud): dbugs 'jug pa'i dusu ni//yi ge thams cad gsad par bya//'byin pa rnams kyis sbyor kun skyes//'byung ba'i dus su shes par bya//gnas dus rnal 'byor
19b7(rgyud): dbang phyug gis//sangs rgyas zhing ni 'dod mi bya//dbugs rgyu de ltar shes bya ste//byis pa'i rnal 'byor pas mi togs//gang gis slob ma dazang skur ba//de nyid 
20a1(rgyud): @#/ O /gzhi rnams bcas pa'o//theg pa chen por yongs smin la//phyi nang sngags ni rab bstan bya//sngon du phag mo'i dkyil 'khor ni//bri byade nas he ru 
20a2(rgyud): ka'i'o//rdo rje'i bu mo'am de nas gzhan//'dir ni shes rab gzhug par bya//rgyud gzhan du ni dbang skur pa//tshul 'di bstan par mi bya'o//ye shes shes bya'i rang bzhin  
20a3(rgyud): can//a ba d+hu tir dkyil 'khor bya//ye shes rnam pa phag mo yis//shes bya dpal he ru kar mkhyud //spyan gsum drag po'i gzugs can ma//zhal bzhi 
20a4(rgyud): mche brtsigs pa ste//phyag ni bcu gnyis rnams dang ldan//dkar mo dang ni 'jigs byed gnon//sku la phyag rgya lnga la sogs//'jig dus me lta'i 'od zer can /
20a5(rgyud): phyag g.yas rdo rje dang gri khug//dgra sta DaM ru de bzhin du//rtse gsum lus med lpags pa ste//phyag g.yon pas kyang de bzhin no//g.yon du dril bu thod pa dang//
20a6(rgyud): zhags pa kha TaM kha dang mgo/dbus skra grol zhing gcer bu ma//mgo bo'i phreng ba 'dzin pa mo//sgeg pa'i rgyan (na)rnams dang ni ldan//dbu la thod pa'i phreng ba can//
20a7(rgyud): 'dod chags rdo rjes rgyas btab ste//yang na he ru ka yis so//yan lag lha rdzas dris nyer byugs//zhags gdub dpung rgyan rnams dang ldan//lha rdzas me
20b1(rgyud): tog phreng bas klubs//rtse bran zlar gnas rdo rje ni//dpral bar rtag tu 'dzin pa mo//g.yas brkyang ba'i zhabs dang ldan//rnal 'byor ma'i tshogs mdun du//sku las 
20b2(rgyud): 'bar ba'i phreng ba spro//he ru ka mig sman nag po 'dra//ral pa'i cod pan mdun du'o//stag gi lpags pa'i sham thabs can//phyag rgya lnga yis rnam par klubs//
20b3(rgyud): dpa' bo thod pa'i phreng ba can//dpa' bo thod pa'i phreng bcan+ye//sna tshogs rdo rjes zla phyed ldan//zhal bzhi pa la 'jigs par byed//phyag bcu gnyis par rig par 
20b4(rgyud): bya||phyag bcu gnyis par rig pa bya//brgya phyed mgo bos brgyan pa ste//mchod phyir thogs pa thal ba dang //'bar ba'i phreng can gcig pa po//skye gnas  
20b5(rgyud): phyag rgya phyag gnyis kyi//lhag ma rdo rje phag mo'i 'o//gal te he ru ka gtso bo//de tshe phag mo'i phag gnyis su'o//glang po'i lpags pa 'dzin pa'o//thabs dang 
20b6(rgyud): shes rab bdag nyid de//zag med rnal 'byor bde chen bzang //srid dang mya ngan 'das pa'i gzugs//gnyis po snying por sbyin par bya//lhag ma rnal 'byor 
20b7(rgyud): ma rnams ni//mkha' gro la sogs rim ji ltar//thod dum la sogs sngags rig pas//dpa' bo kun kyang sbyin par bya//re re ni 'ang de'i rgyud//ji ltar 
21a1(rgyud): @#/ O /sngon gyi rjes su'o//rkyang ma dang ni ro ma'i rtsa//shes rab dang ni thabs 'du ba'i//rten ni a ba d+hu ti te//gang der ro mnyam son pa'o//'khor 
21a2(rgyud): lo gsum gnyis 'gyur zhes bya//mkha'agro ma sogs rim ji bzhin//he ru ka dang rdor phag mo//'bum tsho stong phrag phyag can te//phyag gnyis la sog+sa
21a3(rgyud): nas brtsams nas//gzhan ni gang rnams de'i rnam so//dbang po'i yul ni mi blta zhing //phyag dang bzhugs tshul la sogs (pa)min//bsgom pas spros
21a4(rgyud): pa kun bzung ste//gti mug de ni spang par bgyis//skad cig rnam pa'i rnal 'byor bdag/skad cig la sogs de'i sbyor can//snying khar sa bon rnam par 
21a5(rgyud): dgod//spro dang bsdu ba la sogs bya//dkyil 'khor spyan drang mchod pa dang//sdig pa bshags pa la sogs bya//stong nyid la sogs ri rab mthar//rab 
21a6(rgyud): dang ni gur la sogs//sa'i char ni rdo rje'i thig/'khor lo drug du bri ba'o//ji lta'i mngon rtogs mtshan nyid kyis//rnam pa sna tshogs thams cad bya//thabs dang man 
21a7(rgyud): ngag thams cad ni//mdzes ma bdag gis kun bshad do//der ni gtor ma sbyin bya(ra) ste//na sogs sngags rnams kyis mchod bya//yi ge 'bar pa'i yig rnams kyi(sa)//
21b1(rgyud): sngags kyi bzlas pa yang bya'o//'bar ba'i gnyis 'phel yig rnams kyis//srog gis btum mo sbar bar bya//steng du kha bltas rtsa sreg ste//bde gshegs ye shes
21b2(rgyud): mkha'agro skyes//mkha'agro ma sogs ma lus dang//rnam par shes pa la sogs pas thim//bsregs pas ri bong can haM 'dzag/dpa' bo thams cad mnyam sbyor (ba)//rdo rje sems
21b3(rgyud): dpa' bde ba'i mchog/rdo rje mkha'agro de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan bdag pos 'di gsungaso//dpal mkha'agro rgya mtsho rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po las//
21b4(rgyud): rdo rje phag mo skyed pa dang/gtso mo dang//'khrul 'khor lo dang //dkyil 'khor bsgom pa la sogs pa'i rang bzhin te//'di ni le'u gnyis pa'o////
21b5(rgyud): de nas mkha'agro ma sogs kyis//ji ltar skyed pa rab bshad bya//mkha'agro ma'i sbyor brten nas//bcom ldan 'das ni mchog tu brtson//phag mo'i gzugs
21b6(rgyud): gzugs ni mtha' yas pa 'ang//spros pa 'dra ba'i sdom pa'o//lus kyi dngos po'i dbye ba'i sa//mtha' yas gzugs can mkha'agro ma//lus kyi bde ba
21b7(rgyud): la rten nas//'dod pas rnams pas gsum du 'gyur//dga' ba gsum dang rtsa gsum ste//bzhi pa lhan cig skyes par 'dod//sangs rgyas sku'i nyam chen po//de'i rang 
22a1(rgyud): @#/ O /bzhin las sna tshogs 'phro//mkha' 'gro ma yi gnas brten pa//dkyil 'khor 'khor lo'i rang bzhin no//'dzam bu'i gling ni chen po yi//dum bu bcu //
22a2(rgyud): gnyis kyi dbusu//shar kyi yul na gnas lha mo//dus sbyor chu srin ri hi ha ta chu dang//rgyal dang nyi ma dag bskyed dang//rtsi'i dngos po srog 'bab pa'o//lha mo phyi nang bdag 
22a3(rgyud): nyid du//bcom ldan gzugs can ma gnas te//phyag bzhi ma la kha dog gnag/spyan gsum ma la zhal gcig ma//mche ba brtsigs shing drag po ste//phyag rgya lnga mngon
22a4(rgyud): 'dzin pa mo//ro la zhon pa skra 'grol ma//drag mo thod pa'i phreng can ma//g.yas pa Da ma ru gri gug can//g.yon par thod pa kha T+wAM kha//spro zhing bsdud pa'i sku can ma//suM
22a5(rgyud): bcu rtsa bdun bdag nyid kyi//dkyil 'khor dbusu bzhag par bya//snying po'i yi ge nyi shu gnyis//mkha' 'gro ma dang bcas kha sbyor//A+oM shrI oM badzra he DA he ke tu nA+e 
22a6(rgyud): ru ye kaM hUM Da nI pha dza Ta sa swa b+ha ha ra hUM hUM phaT swAhA////dkyil 'khor ba yi lha mo rnams//yi ge rdo rje can du 'dod//mkha'agro ma tshogs 'joms ma'i mthar//
22a7(rgyud): rig(sa) kyi rtsar ni rab tu grags//mkha' 'gro ma'i grubs pa'i gnas//der ni phag mo gnas par 'gyur//sum bcu rtsa bdun lha rnams ni//rang 'dra'i thabs
22b1(rgyud): dang gnyis med bde//ming gi sbyor bas shes bya ba//dgos pa rnam mang man ngag go//sum bcu rtsa bdun gyi ni rgyud//bde ba che las skyes par 'gyur//gang phyir bdag
22b2(rgyud): po mkha'agro ma//rgyud de mkha'agro ma'i rigaso//bdag po khyed la bdag 'dri'o//ji ltar bdag gi bskyed 'gyur ba//mkha'agro ma la bde ster ba'i//bcom 
22b3(rgyud): ldan 'das ni mnyes byas nas//khyed bskyed pa ni bshad kyis nyon//rang gi rgyud ni ma nyams pa//yi ge bsang ba'i bug bdag nyid//gzhu'i rnam pa srog dang ldan//
22b4(rgyud): lus kyis gzugs can yang dag pa//yul dang dbang po'i bdag nyid can//byang chub sems kyi thig le che//rgyud i zhes bya'i ming can ste//'gro dang 'ong ba'i pad chen
22b5(rgyud): du//rtsa'i 'khor lo la khyab 'gyur//ka yig ka gzugs kyang byed de//bde gsal sprul ba sprul pa'o//phrin las kyi sa ni khams kun gyi//zhi ba'i gzugs kyis 'gro ba che//dga'
22b6(rgyud): ba med mdzes mi 'gyur kyang //brjod min lhan skyes tshon gyis 'tshed//ming gzugs rtag tu bde chen pos//sgyu 'phrul nges par 'phro bar 'gyur//i ni 'byung po che kun dang //dbang phyug la sogs
22b7(rgyud): su ni ston//lug dang dmar ser yid bzang gis//de'i gzhi ni bskyed par 'gyur//'di ni mkha'agro ma'i ming //las kyi dus bzhin mtshon par bya//klu rnams yang dag dgug bya 
23a1(rgyud): @#/ O /dang //char 'beb+sa gzhug phyir bzhu ba dang//lha rnams dang ni 'byung po kun//rgyal po rnams kyi 'ang 'phrul chen dang//dbang dang mngon spyod rengs pa dang //gsad 
23a2(rgyud): dang skra la sogs pa dang//mngon spyod dus la shes par bya//rang rang gling du skyed pa ste//shes rab thabs kyi snyoms 'jug pas//stong nyid snying rje'i bdag can gyi//rnal 'byor
23a3(rgyud): dbang po sems phra zhing //shin tu phra bas der 'jug 'gyur//sems can don du de dang deng//rang bdag sbyor bas mdzad par 'gyur//stong gsum stong ni chen po ru//
23a4(rgyud): gang du khyab bdag kun spyod 'gyur//der ni dum bu bcu gnyis su//rang rang gtso mo rnal 'byor ma//thim dang long+sa spyod spyod rnamasu//rdzu 'phrul che rnam+sa kun spyod gyur//ny+si khri
23a5(rgyud): brgya phrag bcu drug gi/dbugs rgyu bar ni shes par bya//brgya phrag bcwo brgyad srog rnams kyis//'pho bar 'dir ni brjod par bya//bcu gnyis rnams kyis nyid zhag 'gyur//dus
23a6(rgyud): sbyor bcu gnyis rim las so//dus sbyor sum bsgyur byas nas kyang//dus kyi pho nya mo kun spyod //dus kyi phyed gyi pho nya mo//re re rnam par shes par bya//lnga bcu gsum gyi 
23a7(rgyud): yang dag par //nyin zhag gcig la spyod pa'o//de rnams yi ger yang dag bshad//a yig la sogs k+ShA mtha' can//yon tan 'phar ma yin stan pas//gsum 
23b1(rgyud): bsgyur brgya phrag phyed dang bcas//de rnams pho nya mor nges bshad//'di ni mkha' 'gro ma phyag rgya//mkha' spyod ma dang sa spyod gzhan//sa 'og rnams ni 'ang gnas pa mo//
23b2(rgyud): rnal 'byor ma rnams rnam gsum ni//zhing sngags lhan cig skyes rang ngo //gang zhig gang gir lhag gnas pa//de ni de'i gsal mthong 'gyur//rigs kyi khyad par la rten nas//
23b3(rgyud): 'dod pa'i sbyor skyes gnas pa can//tsa ru'i bza' ba de rnamasu//ye shes gzugs can phyugs chen po//bza' min bza' ba der yang ni//mtshon min gsal bya 
23b4(rgyud): mtshon par bya//mtshan nyid mtshan nyid min pa spong //de ni ba rab spong ba'o//rjes 'dzin tshar bcad la changs pa 'ang //gang zhig gang gis rang rang dngos//sangs 
23b5(rgyud): rgyas dang ni chos gnas par byas te mngon spyod sbyor ba yis////a ba d+hu ti'i srog gi rlung //brtan pa phag sogs rnal 'byor ma//ma brtan srog gi rlung rnams kyis//
23b6(rgyud): dngos grub gsal bar mi 'gyur ro//lha mo rnam snang gis rgyas btab//rdo rje 'dzin bdag mi bskyed pa//pho nya mo sngags sum bcu drug/dus la rang dang rang gi rigs//
23b7(rgyud): ji ltar bdag mo'i dbyibs dang ni//phyag dang mtshan ma rim ji ltar//yang na nges par brjod pa ru//mngon rtogs mtshan nyid las shes bya//bo l+la ka k+ko la sbyor bas//
24a1(rgyud): @#/ O /lha mo rang nyid zhu bar zhugs//skad cig de la ma lus pa'i //dkyil 'khor thams cad ro mnyam lta//lha mo namakha'i nang gnas pa//'di yis mnga' 
24a2(rgyud): bdag skul bar bya//mchog tu bde ba chen po'i//rdo rje rol pa cig rol//shes rab thabs kyis dgongs pa grub par mdzod//'jig rten rnams la khyod kyi thugs 
24a3(rgyud): rje sgoms//ji ltar lha dang lha min ma lus sangs rgyas mdzod//byang chub rdo rje 'dzin pas bde chen gyis ni mnyes par mdzod//khyod ni stong nyid ting nge 'dzin la zhugs sam 
24a4(rgyud): ci//rdo rje pad mo rol pas ding ni nges rtogs mdzod//gang gis lhan cig stong nyid ting 'dzin la khyod bzhugs//mnga' bdag khyod kyis khams bzhi sa bon 'dzag par mdzod/
24a5(rgyud): a ba d+hu ti'i rang bzhin gtum mo 'bar bar 'gyur//sa gsum sreg par byed de gang du yang mi mthong ngo//mya ngan 'das pa'i ngo bo 'bar zhing 'bar bar 'gyur//lhan skyes 
24a6(rgyud): rang bzhin gyis ni mkha' 'gro ma rnam(sa) dga' bar mdzod//rga ba dang ni 'chi bar snang ba ma mthong ngo //thugs kyi rgyal ba dgon po yis ni 'di dag sad par mdzod //sgyu mas
24a7(rgyud): sems can khams ni bsgral bar mdzod//glu chen po ni 'di gnas nas//rnal 'byor 'khor lo can rab sad//sgyu ma lta bu'i 'gro ba kun//brtan pa la sog+sa
24b1(rgyud): 'gro ba ni//mthong zhing rang gi 'khor lo can//namakha' thim pa sku chen 'gyur//lhan skyes sogs kyi sprul pa can//gnyis po cig pa'i sku chen po//dang po'i sangs rgyas rang bzhin can/
24b2(rgyud): thams cad kyi ni chos kyi sku//rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud thob nas//rnal 'byor rgyud rnams la yid ches//slob dpon sems kyi rdo rje yang //dbu ma'i tshul gyis spyod yul lo//de nyid phyad
24b3(rgyud): bstan thob pa las//dbu ma chen po'i mtshan nyid do//grub mtha'i man ngag spyod yul ni//mkhas rnams kyi 'ang spyod yul min//grub mtha' mngon sum las shes
24b4(rgyud): bya//man ngag kyang ni rjes dpag las//shes rab bla ma yi zhal las//de mkhas sangs rgyas spyod yulo//byis pa'i rigs kyi las rnams kyis//mngon pa'i nga rgyal spyod yul
24b5(rgyud): min//dpa'o thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i mchog/rdo rje mkha' 'gro de bzhin gshegs//dpal ldan bdag pos 'di gsungaso//zhes bya ba ni
24b6(rgyud): dpal mkha'agro rgya mtsho//rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las//mkha'agro ma skyed pa'i mtshan nyid dang//bde ba la kun tu spyod pa'i las kyi de kho na nyid kyi rnam par
24b7(rgyud): bzhag pa'i cho ga ste//le'u gsum pa'o////de nas yang dag bshad ba bya//lA ma ji ltar gnas pa ni//ji ltar sngon gyi sangs rgyas kyis//gsung+sa
25a1(rgyud): @#/ O /pa (ba)dag gis gsal bya tsam//rdo rje sdom pa bcom ldan 'das//phag mo rnal 'byor sdom pa ste//'khor lo sdom pa mkha'agro ma//de bzhin dam 
25a2(rgyud): tshig sdom pa ni//lA ma yis ni yang dag bstan//pad ma'i sdom par 'dod pa ni//dum skyes ma dang can gzugs can//rin chen sdom pa'o khyab bdag la//lA ma rang
25a3(rgyud): nyid kyis zhus pa//rdo rje sdom sogs ji lta bu//ngo mtshar bdag gi bdag po skye//rdo rje dam tshig de bzhin sbyor//pad 'khor de bzhin rin chen gyi//sdom pa yang ni ji ltar 
25a4(rgyud): lags//de nas rdo rje can mchod nas//mnyes pas bdag la yang dag gsungs//rdo rje bug 'pho med ye shes//rtogs nas rdo rje gsum rnamasu//sdom pa rdo rje gsum 
25a5(rgyud): rnams la//bskyed pa rdo rje sdom pa ni//dam tshig rtsa rnams thams cad kyis//shes bya lam gyi rjes skyob pa'o//sdom pa'i 'khor lo mchod pa ni//dam tshig sdom par 
25a6(rgyud): 'dod pa yin//rnal 'byor rnam pa drug po ni//sbyor drug namakha'i bu kha ru//sdom pa'i bden pa'i dngos po kun//bdag gi rnal 'byor sdom pa'o//pad ma a ba d+hu ti'i
25a7(rgyud): rtsa//'khor lo bzhi la gnas pa ni//sdom pa'i 'khor lo de rnamasu//pad ma'i sdom par brjod par bya//'khor lo bde bar gshegs pa'i lam//dbang sogs rnams
25b1(rgyud): su bskyed pa yis//sdom pa'i chos de rnams su ni//'khor lo sdom pa yin par bshad//rin chen rdo rje mgo bo yis//nor bu 'bab pa 'ongs ba yis//
25b2(rgyud): sdom pa'i srog gi thig le yang //rin chen sdom pa yin par 'dod//ma bshad pa las de rnams kyis//mkha' 'gro bdun pa ye shes mchog/khwa gdong la sogs brgyad 
25b3(rgyud): dang ni//'dren pa gnyis med mtshan nyid phyir//de ltar thams cad mnyam sbyor bas//gcig tu sdom pa mkha' 'gro yin//gtum mo 'bar ba la ni gzugs//rtsa so so dang 
25b4(rgyud): dbu ma ste//ye shes mkha' 'gro de las skyed//rnam pa kun rig skad cig las//byang chub sems kyi dbus su ni//bdud rtsi gsang ba'i gzugs can gang //rdo rje 
25b5(rgyud): mkha' 'gro de bshad do//nam mkhar 'gro ba yi ni mchog/blo bur byung ba bdag med mas//sgyu 'phrul chen po chu skyes gzugs//rnal 'byor rang sems sems med kyang //rnal 'byor 
25b6(rgyud): mkha' 'gro'i mtshan nyid do//'khor lo nang gis bdag nyid gzugs//thig le kun du sbyar ba yi//mtshan nyid gnad dang 'tshams la ni//kun du sangs rgyas skye ba 'gyur//
25b7(rgyud): 'du ba phyi na dang nang bdag nyid//ye shes dngos rang bzhin las so//dam tshig sems dpa' gsum rig pa//'chi dang skye dang bar rnams kyi//srid pa kun las shes par bya/
26a1(rgyud): @#/ O /yongs pas dam tshig mkha'agro skyes//bdag nyid kun gyis mi shes kyang //'gro ba mkhas pa'i lam gyis 'gyur//'dod chags sgyu ma (྾)bcas namakha'//pad ma mkha' 
26a2(rgyud): 'gro rang nyid gtso//dbyangs yig de bzhin rnal 'byor dang //phyag rgya lhan skyes 'dod chags kyi//bgrod bzhin du ni rtag tu skye///'bar ba rin chen dbang mkha'agro/
26a3(rgyud): de bzhin khams (྾)rnam pa bdun//rnam shes bdun gyi mtshan nyid do//chu gter ri dang gling rnams kyang //rgya mtsho che der rab tu zhugs//de nyid nyi shu lnga bdag nyid//sangs rgyas rnam
26a4(rgyud): kyi ye shes rgyus//'khor yul la sogs stong nyid dang//rang bzhin skyes bu de bzhin du//'od gsal ba dang thams cad ming //chos kyi dbyings dang (rang )mtshan nyid//thar dang
26a5(rgyud): sangs rgyas de bzhin du//ye shes rdo rje sems lam bdag/chos 'byung dang ni theg chen dang//gsang ba sngags tshul khyab bdag du//lhan skyes dang ni 'dus de bzhin/
26a6(rgyud): thugs rje chen po'ang rang nyid dang//bdag med pa dang kun de bzhin//rang bzhin can stong yang nas yang //de phyir rgya mtsho'i ming dang yang //rgya mtsho chen po'i gzug+sa
26a7(rgyud): can ni//a kun 'jig gzir sems can rnams//rang sangs rgyas yon tan ldan//b+Tha de rnams dge ba mngon 'dod la//du ba dang yongasu ngal ba med//wa rtog pa tham+sa 
26b1(rgyud): cad spangs pa las//thob pa sgyu ma'i rang mtshan nyid//ma gsang sngags tshul gyi bdag nyid kun//hwa ni bskyed pa'i gzugs can no//sa de'i chos ni mi bden te//mu dngos
26b2(rgyud): po skad cig ('jig)phyir ro//dra rdzas dang sna tshogs rang bstan chos//rgya mtsho chen po'i mtshan nyido//lA ma'i rang gi mtshan nyid sa//ji lta (ba)ba bzhin rim gyis te//rol pa'i mos las
26b3(rgyud): bskyed pa ni//rnal 'byor ma kun shes par bya//ma sogs kun gyi 'khor lo yi//rtsa'i lam nas rjes 'gro ma'o//yang na sgyu ma 'dzin par 'gyur/deni sgyu ma che rang bzhin//bum
26b4(rgyud): pa'i ra si'i spyod pa yi//'pho ba'i grangs kyang gnyis pa'o//rdo rje phag mo can lha mo//kun tu spyod pa lha mor gnas//kha dog ljang nag lha mo ste//lhag ma mkha'agro ma
26b5(rgyud): dang mtshungs//sred pa dang ni sdang ba'i dngos//thams cad de'i steng du gnas//he ru ka'i mdog dang mtshungs//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rjes 'gro rnams//lhag ma 'khor drug rnal 'byor
26b6(rgyud): ma//dpa' bo rnams dang thams cad 'byung //'khor lo'i rtsibs kyi dbusu sbyin //thugs sogs kha dog drug du grags//'khor lo cha mnyam la bri bya//rdo rje pad+ma 'khor de bzhin//
26b7(rgyud): ral gri rin chen gri gug gi//phreng ba rtag tu rim shes bya//phyi rol sna tshogs rdo rje'i phreng //mgo bo'i phreng ba thams cad du'o//lA ma byang gi rigs las bskyed//rnal 'byor ma
27a1(rgyud): @#/ O /tshogs gtso mo ste//dus kyi rjes mthun sems can don//smon lam gyi ni mtshan nyid do//bskal pa bzang po dbusu yang //rdzogs ldan la ni rnams gzig sogs//
27a2(rgyud): de'i dusu shes bya ba//de bzhin rnam gzigs byang gling du'o//'dzam bu'i gling ni chung du na//bdag ni shAkya'i rigs rjes 'gro//lhag ma gling lnga rnamasu ni//de bzhin gshegs rig+sa
27a3(rgyud): rim pas so//de phyir la ma 'gro kun//sangs rgyas mchog gi gnas mtha' len//ma ni rnal 'byor thams cad kyi//snying stobs bya ba byed pa gsal//sa'i dbang phyug zhag la 
27a4(rgyud): ni//mchu yi zla ba can du 'dod//thur sel zhes bya'i rlung dang ni//'gro dang zla ba zhes ba'i rtsa//ji srid 'chi ba'i dus nyid bar//'dzin bzhin du ni mi nyamaso//de ltar rnal 'byor ma
27a5(rgyud): bcu gnyis//zla ba nyin zhag rlung rnams so//rtsa dang rtsa rnams 'bab pas so//mi ni bskyed pa rnal 'byor ma//rtsi'i tshogs ni thams cad la//skad cig las ni srog 
27a6(rgyud): 'pho 'gyur//rang bzhin de las shes byas nas//sdang sogs yon tan gyis rim pas//des ni sna tshogs yul bdag dang//las gyi gnas ni mang ba'o//rnal 'byor 
27a7(rgyud): pa'i dngos gcig ni//lA ma mya ngan 'das mtshan nyid //mya ngan 'das dang mtshon pa na 'ang//gnyis po zhugs 'gyur ming med pa'o//ji ltar mi shes de la ni//bdag gi 
27b1(rgyud): mchog tu 'dus shes so//sangs rgyas med cing byang chub sems dpa' 'ang//chos kyi sku sogs po'o//dbang po rnams kyi drin gyis ni//skye ba can kun thob par 'gyur///
27b2(rgyud): dbang po'i dngos po bden min yang //de tshe mi bden pas thob 'gyur//dbang po gnyis kyis dngos nyid cing //grib ma yin skyes bu mtshungs//grib ma'i dngos po de bden
27b3(rgyud): yang //skyes bu'i gzugs ni bden ma yin//de bzhin dbang po mi bden kyang//de dngos bden ba'i mtshan nyid do//man ngag rnam pa mang po gzhan//mchog tu sngags
27b4(rgyud): pas shes par bya//btung ba la sogs bdo ga ba kun//bla ma la ni dbul ba bden//de ni mi bden pas spyad bya//de la brnyas pas mi bya'o//'di la sngags
27b5(rgyud): kyi nga bshad bya ste//nye ba'i snying po'i sngags rnams la//kha sbyar mdzes pas brgyan pa'o//sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje//nye snying rnams pa gsuM du 'dod//lugs 'byung
27b6(rgyud): lugs las bzlog pa yi//de bzhin dbus su lugs 'byung min//o o bu hi//dzra ha lA he ma hUM ye hUM hUM phaT hUM Da pha Da pha pha hUM Da hUM swAhA ha d+ha hA ha hi/oM oM hUM he hUM hI/
27b7(rgyud): phaT oM zhes bya ba'o//sum bcu rtsa bdun rnams kyis//rnal 'byor dbang phyug ma rim las//rtsa dang tshig ni shes bya ste//yi ge brjod pa'i bdag nyid kyis//gnyis dang
28a1(rgyud): @#/ O /gsum gyi rtsa bskyed de//yi ge re re yi yang ngo//yang na thams cad bsdus pa yis//bdag nyid chen po'i dus 'di'o//de phyir sangs rgyas kun 
28a2(rgyud): bdag nyid//rnal 'byor ma dag re re skye//gal te sems dpa' che zhus nas//spros nas khro bo rnal 'byor ma//de bzhin gti mug 'dod chags sogs//rnal 'byor ma ni 
28a3(rgyud): rtag tu spro//tshegs chung ba yis las grub pa//yum mchog lA ma 'ang thob par 'gyur//bcom ldan dpa' bos 'di gsungs te//yi ge re re rnal 'byor ma//ming ni de 
28a4(rgyud): dang mnyam shes bya//khyod la rdo rje mchog sres pa'o//de ltar de nyid rtag rten nas//rnal 'byor phyag rgya dang lhan cig//brtson cing rgya chen rol bar bya//srid dang mya ngan 
28a5(rgyud): 'das dbus su//dpa' bo thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba mchog/rdo rje mkha' 'gro de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan bdag pos 'di gsung+sa
28a6(rgyud): so//zhes bya ba ni//dpal (mkha')'gro rgya mtsho rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po las//lA ma bskyed pa'i mtshan nyid dang //sngags dgong pa dang//'khor lo bsgyur ba drug
28a7(rgyud): la sogs pa'i rang bzhin dang //mya ngan las 'das pa la sogs pa'i rnam par gzhag pa ste le'u bzhi pa'o////de nas mchog tu rab bshad bya//
28b1(rgyud): sems can (rnams)la phan brtse bas//khan Da ro hi'i g.yas zhugs nas//ma bdag rdo rje mkha' 'gro rang //'khor lo bzhi yi pad mo ru//rtsa ni brgya dang nyi shu'o//de rnam+sa
28b2(rgyud): ming ni ci rigs par//de kho na nyid snod la bzhag/yul dbus ma dang ka ma dang//o Ta ma dang kar+b+na Da me ma ru myo//sA+o raSh+Ta mo ma la ya cing ga ma//'gro lding ma dang
28b3(rgyud): ta ling ga ka mo//mA la wa mo ma hA ra Sh+Thi ma//ba ren Da mo ka ma ru pi ma//Da hA li mo a Ta la yul ma/b+ha Ta ri mo//laDa dbus 'gyur ma//ti ra hu ti mo dang d+ha da ba Na
28b4(rgyud): Da ma dang bal yul ma//gnyan yod ma dang ro D+ha mo dang Tig ka rim dang//bang ga la mo kha Da ma dang ha ri ka la ma//gser gling ma dang sing ga li mo dang//Da ma Ta mo kan te
28b5(rgyud): ra ka mo//sin d+hu kha ba'i gnas ma tsA+au Da mo//ku lu ta mo dza Dar mo pa tha mo//dza dz+dza yul ta ma dang ba ru Na ma dang//u rgyan ma dang lam pa ka ma dang//dza lan d+ha ra ma dang
28b6(rgyud): ar bu ta ma dang//kha che ma dang ko sa mA+o ka ta tsim//dza yan ta mo shri shag ta mo tsam b+ha ma//kha ha ra ma pu ra ho ri ka mo dang//mu mu na ma kla ma dza ma dang//b+ha Do la ma gra ha de
28b7(rgyud): wa mo//pre ta pu ri mo dang bab b+ha ra ma dang//si la mo dang nye ba'i sil mo//dur khrod ma dang nye ba'i dur khrod ma//rgya mtsho chen po'i 'gram ma kha sa mo//kla klo ma
29a1(rgyud): @#//dang thams cad yul ma ste//rim pa drug bcu bzhi lha mo//lte ba'i 'khor lo rnal 'byor ma//rigs kyi rtsar ni shes par bya//snying kha'i 'khor lor 
29a2(rgyud): de bzhin brgyad //pho nya mo kun 'gro ma ste//pra yA ga mo de wa ka Ta ma//'phags rgyal ma dad dpal chen mo//'bar ba'i gdong ma sim pa li mo dang //
29a3(rgyud): ma hi la ma kA+au ma rim sA+au ra ma//de ltar thams cad snying gnas su//sgyu ma'i rnam pa rang zhi ma'o//'grin pa'i 'khor lor lha mo yang //gtso mo mchog ni bshad 
29a4(rgyud): par bya//cha chen bcu drug nyid yin te//khams rnams bcu drug rang bzhin no/////khrag dang khu ba rkang dang ni//rdul dang tshil dang lpags pa dang //
29a5(rgyud): sha dang rus pa rgyus pa dang //rnag dang sgyu ma rang 'byung dang //gshang gci 'khris pa mchil ma mo'o//ra ha s+ya sogs tshig gi khams//der rnam myos byed 'bab
29a6(rgyud): ma'o//mgo bo ru ni lha mo che//'khor lo sum bcu rtsa gnyis lha//zhag gi rim pa rims cad du//tshangs kun rmad 'byung chen ma'o//nag mo gtsigs ma
29a7(rgyud): 'jigs rung ma//dga' ba 'das ma log 'dren ma//lha mo dka' zlog dbyangs can ma//nag bzang ma dang nag chen mo//nag sbom ma dang gzhan ma thub//
29b1(rgyud): rgyal ma rnam par rgyal ma dang//mi pham mo dang rgyal ba mo//zla ba lam gyi bzhi mdo ma//grong gnas ma dang drag mig ma//kam mo dza ma DoM bi ma//gtum mo dang
29b2(rgyud): ni gdol pa mo//bram ze ma dang rmangs rigs mo//rgyal po'i bu mo 'phrul chen mo//lha rdzas chang gis dgengs byed ma'o//de ltar rtsa'i 'khor lo ru//bdag
29b3(rgyud): 'dir 'gro ba mo yin cing//rang gi bdag pos bka' stsol cig/lus (nas)thams cad la ni khams//mkha'agro dngos po cig tu 'gyur//sa bzhi'i cha bzhin shes bya
29b4(rgyud): ste//yul gyis phye yang sa gzhi cig/dum bu dum bur gyur pa'i//Di'i yige bstan de'i phyir//sgong nga'i mngal gyi mtshan nyid las//'dir ni rnam shes bskyed
29b5(rgyud): par 'gyur//gang 'dra'i gang gi khams rnams la//bzhir ni rnam par shes bya ste//las la dum skyes ma rang nyid//sdig pa chen po 'phrog bzhin pa//
29b6(rgyud): gsang la khams ni thams cad do//des ni sems can dral dum skyes//shin du las kun mdzad pa mo//gtso mo mchog gi rnal 'byor ma//rang gi 'pho ba
29b7(rgyud): las bzhengs mchog/nya ni 'char ba'i dus dang ni//'dod dang rgyu skar dro mchog dang//nub gyi ba glang spyod gling du//'dod chags chen mo'i rang bzhin ma//dngos  
30a1(rgyud): @#/ O /med byed pa med lha mo//gnyis med go 'phang la rten ma//sum bcu bdun bdag nyid dbus su//bskyed 'gyur rang gi sngags las skye//oM badzra
30a2(rgyud): sarba kha bu NA d+ha re ba hi dza ka Ta ye ke hUM ni/hUM ye hUM ye phaT phaT//r+N+Na sa ni ha ye hUM hUM phaT phaT swAhA oM//sngags 'di rab tu sbyor ba yis//dkyil 'khor 'dod pa thams cad 
30a3(rgyud): pa//lha mo re re rnams kyi ni//dbus su 'khor lo bzhi skyes pa//thabs dang shes rab bdag nyid can//thams cad chos 'byung dbus su te//sgo bzhi dang ni yang 
30a4(rgyud): dag ldan//rta babs kyis ni de rnams brgyan//sha chen po ni za bzhin du//'jigs byed sgra ni sgrags pa'o//lA ma'i 'khor lor ba la sha//mkha' 'gro 
30a5(rgyud): la ma glang chen sha//phag mo la ni sha thams cad//dam tshig sdom pa'i sbyor bas so////gsang sngags bden par shes byas nas//sngags kyis yi ge
30a6(rgyud): brjod pa min//sems can kun gyi ngag gang de//sngags ni rnal 'byor ma ru grag/gal te yi ge pa sngags yin//go ( )ri'i yi ge pa dang mtshungs//rtsa'i bu ga bu
30a7(rgyud): ga ru/dbugs ni yi ger 'du shes pa'o//sngags kyi btung bas sems la ni//ji srid rkang mthar 'gog pa las//yul dang yul du kun tu spyod /
30b1(rgyud): yul dbus su yang 'ongs ba las//tshim bya rang gi 'khrul 'khor du//bum pa bzhin du btung ba dbab//'khrul 'khor rang gi bstan nas//srog gi༐
30b2(rgyud): 'khor lo bzhin du bskor//sems dpas 'khor bzhin pa ru ni//nor bu'i bu gar btsir bar bya//bu ga dgu ru sa bon kyang //rdo rje sogs pas bsgom bar bya//yang 
30b3(rgyud): na rtsa rnams thams cad du//sngags kyi yi ge 'di dgod bya//ma ka o ka ma sA+au ma tra ta ma mA b+ha ka Da a b+ha lA ma ti da ne prA ra Di ba khA sa ha su si Da ka si ti bu ku dza ya dza ba
30b4(rgyud): o liM dza a kA ko kAM dza tri//tsa lu yu muM kaM b+ha gre pre ba pe u ma kha me//zhes bya ba 'di ni lte bar ro//bri de u ma dzA si ma kau//zhes bya ba ni snying khar ro//ra su ma swe tsa maM 
30b5(rgyud): a snA su a swA bi mu pi shI//zhes bya ba ni mgrin pa'i 'o//kri ka b+hI na ti bi tsa o swA b+ha ma s+thu a dza go du i tsa tsa gra rA+au kaM tau tsa ma brA ba ru//zhes bya ba ni mgo bo'i 'khor
30b6(rgyud): lo la'o//yang na mi phyed pa la sogs pa'i ming can te//de rnams kyi yang yi ge dang po//thams cad mgo bo'i 'khor lor sbyin//'khrul 'khor 'di ni bla chen po//
30b7(rgyud): srog gi rlung ni rnam 'dzin 'gyur//pad ma bzhi'i 'dab ma'i dbyibs//re'u mig 'khor lo'i rim thig gdab//thams cad zlum po'i rnam par ni//thig lnga rnams ni 
31a1(rgyud): @#/ O /gdab par bya//brgyad dang bcu drug bcu bzhi ste//sum bcu rtsa gnyis re'u mig 'dod//lta ba'i gnas ni thob nas su//srog gi nus pa rnam+sa
31a2(rgyud): kyis 'khor//ji srid a ba d+hu ti'i rlung //de srid 'khrul 'khor 'di brjod do//gal te ngag gis de ltar byed//sngags ni 'jam snyan gyis glu len//sgras ni srog des 
31a3(rgyud): phur btab nas//rtsa dgu po ni dgag par bya//nad med mi rgas mdze med cing //rtag tu skres dang skom pa med//de ltar la sogs mtha' yas pa//nad 'chi ba'i mtha'
31a4(rgyud): thug med//dkyil 'khor snga raM yang na ni//'khrul 'khor can kun 'bab par bya//khams dang skye mched gzhan la sogs//bdud rtsi'i rnal 'byor ldan 
31a5(rgyud): pas bza'//mdog dmar 'du shes lhag ma ni//la'i ma'i phyag dang gzugs can no//dpa' bo thams cad mnyam sbyor ba//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba mchog/rdo rje 
31a6(rgyud): sems dpa' bde ba mchog//rdo rje mkha'agro de bzhin gshegs/bcom ldan bdag pos 'di gsungaso//zhes bya ba ni/dpal mkha'agro rgya mtsho rnal 'byor
31a7(rgyud): ma'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po las//duM skyes ma'i mtshan nyid dang //'khor lo bzhi'i rtsa'i rnam par bzhag pa'i ming mdor bstan pa dang //sngags dgong pa la sogs
31b1(rgyud): pa'i cho ga ste////le'u lnga pa'o////de nas gzhan mchog bshad bya ba/mdor bsdus pa ste rgyas par min//dpal he ru ka'i ka sbyong las//
31b2(rgyud): gzugs can ma'i mtshan nyid dge/lus ldan dang ni las dang tshigs//sngon du gzugs can ma la rten//phyi nang bdag nyid can gzugasu//bde la gnas pas 
31b3(rgyud): 'grol bar 'gyur//'dir ni rkang lag thams cad la//tshigs ni bcu gnyis nyid yin te//lnga lnga ru ni shes bya ste//bcu gnyis la rtsa drug bcu'o dkyil 'khor rnams
31b4(rgyud): la 'ang 'bab pa mo//'pho ba bcu gnyis su 'dod do//g.yon pa dang ni g.yas pa la'ang//sngon du lugs sogs sngags rigs pas//lhag ma bu ga bzhi rnamasu lte ba
31b5(rgyud): 'doms dang rtsib logs gnyis//gru gsum de bzhin shes bya ste//de'i sgo rnams rtsa drug bcu//'tshams ni rnam pa lnga rab gsungs//nyi ma rgyu bar gang 'gro 
31b6(rgyud): ba//de 'gro zla ba zla rnamasu//drug lnga sum bcu'i cha rnams so//snying khar grags pa de bzhin gsungs//rtsa dgu ji ltar 'gro ba'o//mig la sogs pa'i bug rnamasu//
31b7(rgyud): sgo la rten na gnas par 'gyur//mgrin par gang 'dra ba yi khams//de ru gyur nas gnas pa'o//mi phyed ma la sog sum bcu gnyis//rgyud mang por ni sngags 
32a1(rgyud): @#/ O /pa'o//de'i gzugs lha mo//gzugs can de'i rnam rtog pas//bcom ldan 'das la bdag 'dri ba'o//gzugs can ming ming ci gsung+sa/
32a2(rgyud): 'gro ba thams cad gzugs med mdzod//rtsa'i bde sogs ming giso//'gro ba 'ang po'i lam rnamasu//gang 'dra'i gzugs kyi gzugs can ma//pha las
32a3(rgyud): thob pa'i sbyor bas so//dbang gnyis pa'i sbyor bas so//gang dang gang gi rig gang ni//de dang de'i 'gro bar bshad//bden pa kun las 'dren par 'gyur//sngags
32a4(rgyud): kyi tshul las gzhan mi 'gro//de phyir gzugs can ma 'gog pas//lhan skyes rdzogs pa'i byang chub tshol//lha mo chen mo kha dog ser //lhag ma dum skyes 
32a5(rgyud): ma dang mtshungs//'du byed phung po'i gzugs dang ni//mi dang bud med sogs gzugs can//mnyam med sems can don las phyir //gling 'di ru ni skye 'gyuro//lug
32a6(rgyud): dang spyang ki la nag pa'i//zla ba ra si'i rang bzhin can//thabs dang shes rab bde bas kyang //dkyil 'khor dbus su bzhugs pa'o//yang dag sum bcu bdun bdag
32a7(rgyud): nyid //ji ltar rim pas dpa' rnal 'byor//gsang sngags kha sbyor sbyor ba ru//gzugs can gtso mo yis shes par bya//oM badzra graM ru ma pi ma//ni hi ya o ke swA
32b1(rgyud): hUM hA hUM hUM hUM//pha oM Da bA+au pha Sha Ta he swA hA hU hUM haM ho//oM phaT phaT haM//de lta rang bzhin las shes bya//sngon gyi cho ga'i las kyis ni//dbu ma'i bu gar phyin nas ni//dbug+sa
32b2(rgyud): ni rnam shes ngo bo can//sangs rgyas zhing ni grangs med pa//ma lus skad cig de la mthong //ma lus rang bdag ngo bo can//bsgom dang thos dang mthong ba
32b3(rgyud): yang/bgrod bzhin phan rtogs mi 'gyur //'di ni sgyu ma'i rang mtshan nyid//gzugs kyi sku yang rnams gsum ste//sku gsung thugs kyi mtshan nyid do//de ni
32b4(rgyud): 'dod dang gzugs gzugs med//me long du ni gdong rdzogs pa//ji bzhin khams gsum la gnas nas//de'i gzugs dang gzhan gzugs can//
32b5(rgyud): gzugs dang gzugs gzugs brnyan te//grangs med pa yi 'jig rten khams//gzugs can sgyu mas bud med dang //thabs ni skyes bu'i las can gyi//gnyis
32b6(rgyud): med bud med skyes bu cig/gcig min gcig min gzugs can//gzugs kyi yul la sogs dngos la//mtshon byed mtshon bya med gzugs can
32b7(rgyud): mtshon bya dga' ba mtshon bya'o//mtshon cha gzugs med mtshan nyid ma//mtshon med spyod yul de yang ni//mtshon pa po de'i spyod yulo//ji ltar ji ltar rmi lam mthong //rang nyid
33a1(rgyud): @#/ O /rang nyid shi ba mthong gyur na'ang//mthong bzhin du yang du bar byed//gzugs dang gzhan gyi gzugs can no//de ltar kun gyi rang bzhin du //rdo rje 
33a2(rgyud): sems dpa' rang gi dang//sems dpa' chen po'i rang bzhin dang//dam tshig sems dpa' chen po la/byang chub chen po'i sem+sa sems dpa'/brtan dang 'gro ba sna tshogs
33a3(rgyud): pa'o//de lta'i gzugs ni ji ltar sngon//rtog ge pa rnams kyis mi shes//byis pa rnal 'byor rtogs bya min//rnal 'byor ma yi mchog thob pas//skye ba 'di nyid l+sa 
33a4(rgyud): shes 'gyur//skye bo thams cad byang chub rang bzhin te//rmongs pa'i byis pas shes pa ma yin no//sems can thams cad rnal 'byor ma de nyid //ming gang yin 
33a5(rgyud): pa de ni rdo rje 'chang//glu 'di blangs pas thams cad ni//skrag te sa la rgyal bar 'gyur//rnal 'byor ma rnams dgyel nas kyang//chos kyi spyod yul ngo mtshar te/
33a6(rgyud): thod pa'i dum bu la sogs btso//dpa' bo rnams kyi(sa) bskul mdzad nas//gson cig gson cig sring mo khyed//sems can khams kyi rang bzhin 
33a7(rgyud): gol byed ma//khyed dang sems can rnams la dbye ba med//dpa' bo thams cad sgrol bar mdzad pa mo//slar yang bcom ldan 'das kyis ni//skul thams
33b1(rgyud): cad rab tu zhugs//slar yang spro ba'i sbyor ba yis//gtso mo zhal nas spros par 'gyur//rnam snang mdzad la sogs pa'i rgyud//slar yang zhal nas
33b2(rgyud): spro bar mdzad//bcom ldan thams cad bsdu mdzad nas//rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud la ni//ched du brjod pa ched brjod mdzad//rnal 'byor rgyud kun la yang ngo//
33b3(rgyud): rnam snang mdzad dang rin chen dang//'dir ni 'dod chags don yod grub//mi skyod pa dang spyan ma'i rgyu//ma ma ki dang gos dkar mo//sgrol ma dang ni gzugs rdo rje/
33b4(rgyud): sgra dang dri dang ro de bzhin//reg dang chos dbyingasu grags dang//sa snying namakha'i snying po dang//phyag na rdo rje 'jig rten dbang//sgrib kun sel dang bzang po
33b5(rgyud): dang//'jam dpal dang ni byams pa dang//gshin rje dgra dang shes mthar mdzad//pad ma mthar mdzad dges mthar mdzad//mi g.yo ba dang dbyug sngon can/
33b6(rgyud): 'dod dang stobs chen gnod mdzes dang//gtsug tor 'khor lo sgyur ba dang//khro gnyer ri khrod lo ma can//lhag ma rdo rje sems dpa'o//de ltar rgyud kyi ming
33b7(rgyud): don ni//thabs kyi dngos po'i mtshan nyido//shes rab rgyud ni rang 'byung nyid//rang+ga rang bzhin rtag tu gnas//byang chub phyogs kyi chos rnamasu//mi 'jigs
34a1(rgyud): @#/ O /go 'phang rjes 'gro ba//bskal pa bsreg pa'i dus su ni//gzhan du Isong nas yang dag gnas//thod pa'i dum sogs dpa'o yi//ming 
34a2(rgyud): ni mi skyed la sogs pa'o//dngos po dbye ba'i rnam grangs kyis//rigs ni du ma ( )byung ba'o//gzugs can ma'i gnas brten nas//dpa' bo sa la 'gying bar 'gyur/
34a3(rgyud): gzugs ni gzugs med gyur pa'i gzugs//bde mchog dang ni mnyam par 'khyer //dpa' bo thams cad mnyam sbyor bas//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i 
34a4(rgyud): mchog/rdo rje mkha'agro de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan bdag pos 'di gsungs so//zhes bya ba ni/dpal mkha'agro rgya mtsho rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud kyi rgyal po 
34a5(rgyud): chen po las//gzugs can ma'i mtshan nyid kyi rang bzhin//rtsa'i 'khor lo'i rang bzhin dang//gnas kyi rnam par bzhag pa dang//rgyud kyi mtshan nyid kyi chog 
34a6(rgyud): ste/le'u drug pa'o////de nas rnal 'byor ma mchog gis//cho ga mthong ba'i las kyis te//gang gis kha gdong ma 'byung ba//de'i tshe gas 
34a7(rgyud): thar 'gro ba//nye ba'i gling du gnas ma ste//khyu mchog 'pho dang bzang mo dang//sa ga'i zla bar shes bya ste//sa lnga par ni 'dod pa yin//lha mo sgo na gnas pa 
34b1(rgyud): /mo//ka ni chos dbyings mar 'du shes//gnyis pa chos kyi sku dang ni//de bas longs spyod rdzogs 'byung ba//s+yA ni sprul pa'i sku yin te//lte snying 'grin pa sbyi bo las//bu ga
34b2(rgyud): bzhi rnams shes bya ste//bya rog 'dra ba'i srog gdong can//de la rtag tu mngon spyod ni//srog chags rnams ni skad cig gsod//klu rnams dgug par bya ba dang //char 'bab
34b3(rgyud): pa dang rengs pa'o//chos kyi dbyings dang byang chub che//rnal 'byor ma kun rang bzhin can//chos dang long spyod sprul pa la//bde ba 'dod pas 'gro ba mo//sngags 
34b4(rgyud): bden las kyi bdag nyid can//bya rog gtso mo de la rten//rdo rje phag mo de la ni//bya rog ma ni gnas su son//sum bcu rtsa bdun rnal 'byor mas//sngags 'di las ni 'byung
34b5(rgyud): ba yin//oM ba na badzra mo kA b+ha kA ga s+yA ba ya te ke be hUM re hUM shwa sa ma Da hUM pha hUM Ta pha swA Da ha la o Da swA hUM ha hUM//'di ni ring thung dbye ba las//shes rab thabs kyi rang bzhin can/
34b6(rgyud): thung du thabs kyi rang bzhin dang //thung du thabs kyi rang bzhinadang //ring po shes rab mngon par brjod//kha dog nag mo drag chen mo//lha mo mkha'agro ma dang mtshungs//gang
34b7(rgyud): phyir byang chub sems zhu ba//de tshe bya rog las rgyal mo//namakha' bya rog bzhin 'khor ba//bya rog gdong ma'i rlung gis su//gang phyir 'phur zhing ster 'phur ba//
35a1(rgyud): /de phyir 'go dang steng du blta//bya rog bzhin du ring 'phur nas//bya rog gling du slar 'dos pa'o//der son pa ni bde chen po//lus kyis nyams su 
35a2(rgyud): myong ba'o//bsod nams las kyis skye bar 'gyur//rnal 'byor gong nas gong du'o//bsod nams mi ldan de bzhin nyid//dmyal ba bya rog dum bu can//pha rol phyin 
35a3(rgyud): tshul pa rnams kyis 'ang //sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin par 'dod//ma byin len las dmyal ba ba//bya rog bzhan du rtag tu bkres//de'i de ltar las dus su//mi gang dang 
35a4(rgyud): gis 'chi bar 'gyur//rnal 'byoram ni de bsgoms nas//bde chen nyams su myong bar 'gyur//rnal 'byor mi'i spyod kun kyang //rang gis rang ni theb par 'gyur//las ma 
35a5(rgyud): byas na gang gis ni//las can med cing las byed med//rang sems 'pho ba las yin te//las de las byed pa yang de//lus kyi dbyibs dang gzugs bdag nyid//sems 
35a6(rgyud): med pa ni sems pa mchog//lag pa rkang pa thams cad kyis//gang byas las byed pa rang ngo //de kun gyi nar khyer ga ni//yang dag las mtha'i mtshan nyid do//las 
35a7(rgyud): kyang dge ba rgyu don gnyer//mi dge rang gis spang bar bya//rnal 'byor ma rnams kyis mchod pa 'am//de bzhin bza' ba 'du ba ni//grong dang grong khyer gnas pa dang //
35b1(rgyud): rig la sogs pa'i rtse chen du//dam tshig don du mchod par bya//rnal 'byor dngos grub 'bras rab ster//mkha' 'gro ma'i gzugs brnyan kyang //khyab gnas rlung 
35b2(rgyud): gi ming can te srog dang thur sel mnyam gnas dang //khyab byed ji lta'i rim nyid kyis//mkha' 'gro de bzhin lA ma dang //dum skyes ma dang gzugs can ma//'ug  
35b3(rgyud): gdong khyi dang phag gdong ma//rim pas klu dang ru(rgyal)rtsangs//lhas sbyin dang ni nor lhas rgyas//rtag tu rtan pa pho nya gnyis//dga' dang skye ba zhes bya ba//mche ba dang ni 
35b4(rgyud): 'joms ma mchog/mdor stan pa'i tshig 'di yang //rgyas bshad kun nas brjod par bya//phag mo'i mchod pa byas nas ni//rdo rje sems dpa' la zhus pa/
35b5(rgyud): ji srid srog rtsol rlung rnams kyi(sa)//so so'i re'u yi ming 'du shes//bdag gis ston pa ngo mtshar che//dus kun du ni skye bar 'gyur//bcom ldan 'das kyis
