35b6(rgyud): bka' stsal pa//dang po(ra) gtso bo can 'dzin pa//stong pa'i ngo bos gol bar 'gyur//rdo rje lta bu'i rlung dgong bya//rten ni snod kyi 'jig rten la'o//gang phying
35b7(rgyud): 'jig pa'i thim 'gyur ba//de'i dbyibs tshul kyang med pa//de phyir thur sel zhes bya yang //'gro ba dang ni 'ong bar bstan//mnyam gnas rigasu son nas ni//
36a1(rgyud): @#//sngon du gang dang gang rnam kyis //ngag tu brjod pa smra bar 'gyur//sngon du bag chags ma lus pa'o//khyab byed 'gro ba thams cad la//rgyas 'debs bas 
36a2(rgyud): na las kyi rlung//klu ni khro bo'i ngo bo nyid/khyab byad de bzhin tshig gnyis te//ru sbal ser sna'i ngo bo ni//smra ba yi ni mtshan nyid do//rtsangs pa 'dod chags 
36a3(rgyud): mnyams gnas so//thur ser phrag dog lhas sbyin no//nor rgyal gti mug bdag nyid de//srog gi rlung ni grangs gnyis las//dga' ba'i ye shes can zhes
36a4(rgyud): bya//skye bo de rtag pa'o//de phyir mkhas pas so sor shes pa yis//gang phyir srog gi rlung ni thams cad bdag nyid skye//ji ltar sa ni bcu gnyis las//
36a5(rgyud): thog ma de nyid bcu gnyis pa'o//bcu po rnams kyis de shes bya//gang phyir sa bcu'i dbang phyug go/sa gcig rang bzhin rnams kyis ni//rdo rje 'dzin pa chen  
36a6(rgyud): mor 'dod//sbyin sogs pha rol phyin pa gang//lhan skyes yang dag byang chub gnyis//thams cad kyis ni nang son pas//rdo rje dbyings dbang phyug ma mchog/bshad
36a7(rgyud): dang ma bshad gang ci 'ang rung //de kun byang chub tshul can no//slar yang bya rog dbyibs lha mo//de ni byed pa'i gzugs can ma'o//byed pa'i phyog+sa kyi gnas la ste//
36b1(rgyud): rtsa rnams kun gyi bu ga'o//thim dang de las langs bsam gtan//khA gdong ming du 'du shes pa'o//de ltar rnal 'byor thams cad ster//bla ma'i zhal las gsal thob
36b2(rgyud): 'gyur//bla ma gang de mi bsnyas shing //ston byas de nyid 'dod par bya//'phags pa'i go 'phang ma thob na//mnar med la sogs rtag tu 'tshed//kun nas bla ma gus pa 
36b3(rgyud): las//byang chub bar du thob par 'gyur//dpa' bo thams cad mnyam sbyor bas//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i mchog/rdo rje mkha'agro de bzhin gshegs//bcom
36b4(rgyud): lhan bdag pos 'di gsungaso//zhes bya ba ni//dpal mkha'agro rgya mtsho rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po las//kha gdong ma la sogs pa'i srog bskyed pa'i mtshan
36b5(rgyud): nyid kyi cho ga ste/le'u bdun pa'o////de nas gzhan gyi mtshan nyid mchog/'ug gdong la sogs gang gsal ba//byed pa yang dag bya ba dang //thams
36b6(rgyud): cad 'tshams ni bshad par bya//'khrig pa'i sdig pa sbron rnams kyis//zla ba'i sbyor bas rtag tu gnas//'ug gdong ye shes thob nas ni//rtsa ni lam gsum 'gro ba mo//
36b7(rgyud): u ni drag shul gzi brjid gtsigs pa mo//ye shes lam dbang bza' ba las//'u 'dod pa dga' sgyu 'phrul che//lha min lha sogs skye rnams la//rkang pa'i 'theb
37a1(rgyud): @#/ O /mthar bcu gnyis su//ka 'di'i shugs kyis 'gro bar 'gyur//'ug gdong ma'i ming la yang//u ni u rgyan gar 'dod //la phur l+lA ma ra ma la yang grag+sa/
37a2(rgyud): u ni mu ru ni zhing ngo//kA na kA ma rU pa ste/de bzhin s+yA 'dir zhing kun 'dod//zhing kun dbus su gyur pa ni//gru gsum rnam pa'i bde ba'i mtha'//gang phyir gnas lnga'i bdag
37a3(rgyud): nyid kyi//mi bzad he ru ka'i go 'phang/yang na bdag gi gdan sogs su//dpal he ru ka rdo rje rang//skye gnas gnas lnga'i bdag nyid do//snying lte rkan dang mgo bo
37a4(rgyud): dang /gsang ba ru yang rtsa gsum gyi//skye gnas bu gar lnga bdag nyid //sgong skyes dang ni drod gsher skyes//mngal skyes rdzus te skyes pa rnams//sems can sangs 
37a5(rgyud): rgyas yon tan bdag/rigs lngar btu ston pa'o//nye ba'i gling du gnas ma yang//gtso mo gzugs can yang dag gnas//sngags kyi phreng ba las skyes rnams/sum 
37a6(rgyud): bcu rtsa bdun bdag nyid skyes//oM ba ma dzra ha u ga lU pa lA ka a s+yA gi ya saM ke ni hUM/b+ha hUM ya phaT hUM Da hUM pha pha Da Da swA pha Ta oM swa hUM ha/de ltar sngags de kho na'i 
37a7(rgyud): bdag/shes rab thabs kyi rang bzhin cad//kha dog ljang ser drag chen mo//lhag ma la ma dad ni mtshungs//dkyil 'khor dbus su sgom byas nas//dur khro gnas dang gnyis 
37b1(rgyud): med pa//kun spyod rtsa rnams thams cad kyi//rgyas pas dga' ba'i spyod yul lo rnams thams cad kyi//'di ni 'tsho ba'i ting nge 'dzin dang//'od gsal rnams pa'i chos nyid de//
37b2(rgyud): bskrad pa'i las dang rnal 'byor ma//'jig rten gyi ni 'dren pa mo//rang stan chos las gsungs pa yi//sna tshogs las kyang byab mo//kun gyi rnal 'byor yongasu rdzogs
37b3(rgyud): nas//rgyud 'di yi ni spyod yul lo//'on kyang rnal 'byor de kun kyang//mdor bsdus tshul ni tshig 'di yin//so sor bsdud dang de bzhin du//bsam gtan 
37b4(rgyud): srog rtsol 'dzin pa dang//rjesu dran dang ting 'dzin de//sbyor ba'i mtshan nyid rnam drug go/so sor bsdud pa kha gdong ma//'ug gdong ma ni gsam gtan 'dod//
37b5(rgyud): srog rtsol du ni phag mo'o//rjes su dran gnas pa pho nya mo//ting nge 'dzin brtan ma 'dod//mche ba 'di ni rgyas bstan thob//'joms mas 'bras bu kun bdag skyes//'khor
37b6(rgyud): ba la ni grol gyur pa//rang gi sku ni sgyu ma mnyam//bdag byin rlabs 'di phra mo dang//rdo rje bzlas rang mtshan nyid do//rdzogs pa'i byang chub sems kyi gter//de las lhag
37b7(rgyud): ma zung 'jugo///mya ngan 'das zhes bya ba'ang 'di//lam thams cad kyis mya ngan 'das//theg chen kun la rab 'jug pa//sngags kyi theg pa ru ni byed//bya ba bstan bcos dang//
38a1(rgyud): @#/ O /sna tshogs//snying rje mnyes gshin pa yis byed//gang 'dra'i dngos dang dngos (pa)la//ye shes rnam par rang 'byung gis//de 'dra ba yi sems rgyun las//
38a2(rgyud): bdag nyid rnal 'byor rnams kyis mthong//lus kyi mtshan nyid yang dag pa//rtsa'i gur ni bshad par bya//spyi tsugs nas ni nor bu'i mthar//'tshams dang mgrin pa rnam gnyi(sa)
38a3(rgyud): 'dod//rkang pa'i mthe 'ong mtshams mtha' dang//smin dbus de bzhin sgro bste//mtshan ma'i rtsa ba bral ba dag/mig dang rkang pa'i sor mo gnyis//ming med dang
38a4(rgyud): ni dbus mor shes//byin pa rnam gnyis dag dang//rkang pa'i mthil dang snying ka dang//lte ba dang ni rgyab gzhung dag/gshang lam dang ni rkan shes bya//rkang pa'i tshig 
38a5(rgyud): dang( )mchu dag dang//'khur ba 'khur tshogs dag dang ni//'doms dang mchan khung gnyis dang ni//lag pa'i tshigs dang rkang pa dag/dpung pa'i tshigs dang nu ma gnyis//'jing
38a6(rgyud): pa thal gong 'dir 'tshams so//mjug ma nor bu gnyis dag dang//rked pa'i tshig dang mgrin pa dang//dpral ba dang ni tshig(sa) gnyis dag/rna bu dag dang rtsa ba dang//
38a7(rgyud): brang dag gnyis kyi 'tshamasu 'dod//stan dag dang ni smin khugs dang//lce da mi'i sna gang dag/de bzhin du ni rkan dang lce//bo l+lA ka k+ko la gnyis dag/de la sog+sa
38b1(rgyud): pa du ma ni//rtsa dang tshigs kyi gnad du 'gyur//rang rang spyod la btsir bya ste//byang sems bdag nyid che rnams kyis//btsan thabs sbyor ba'i cho ga ru//shes bya bla ma'i 
38b2(rgyud): gsung gis su//zla ba zla ba'i tshes gang du//tshes grangs de la rtsa ba'i rtsa//dkar po'i tshes gcig la sogs nas//ji srid nag po nag po mthar thug pa//shes bya byang 
38b3(rgyud): chub 'bab pa'i rtsa//'ug mo sgo la spyod pa mo//gang dang 'dra ba'i gdong de yi//de 'dra'i sor me'i phyag rgya ni//dbus sa ni de dang 'dra bar bya//phyi dang nang gi bdag nyid 
38b4(rgyud): 'dod//yang na rtsa rnams thams cad ni//brtsir bya bcu gnyis kyis lhag pa'i//brgya'i tshogs rnams thams cad ni//ji ltar gdong gi rim pas so//de dang de ni a la 'bar//
38b5(rgyud): drag po'i sgyu ma dus kun du//de phyir a zhes bya ba'i sgo//tshig du rtogs pas rtags pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i dbugs dbyung bar//rdo rje 'chang ltar brtag pa'o//
38b6(rgyud): gang dang gang gis 'ching 'gyur ba//rtsa ni gti mug chen por 'gyur//de yis de yi rang bzhin 'gyur//sna tshogs gzugs kyi rin chen bzhin//rmongs dang mkhas rnams
38b7(rgyud): kyis ngag la//mnga' bdag thar pa chen po bzhugs//byed pa 'byung po thams cad kyi//dbang phyug skye bo sna tshogs rnams//de phyir rtag tu lam nyams pa//sngon byas sdig

39a3(rgyud): 'ug gdong ma'i nges pa'i tshul dang//yang dag pa'i cho ga las bskyed pa dang//srog la sogs pa'i mtshan nyid de//le'u ste brgyad pa'o////de nas gzhan ni don che ba/

39a5(rgyud): bar 'gyur//dus kun du ni gnyid log dang//sad pa bde ba'i yon tan che//na sna tshogs gnas su song nas ni//bdag nyid kyis ni bde rigs 'gyur//brgya las nyi shus lhag
39a6(rgyud): pa ni//des tshig bstan bya mtshan nyid do//de ltar khyi'i ming du ni//bde ba sbyin pa bor shes bya//pad ma'i skye gnas de rnamasu//a s+yA/pad ma'i kha ni rab rgyas pa//gtang dang long+sa
39a7(rgyud): spyod gar chen po//klu dang rol mo gnyid log dang//reg dang gos dang rgyan dag dang//me tog la sogs pa gsung can 'dod//mthun pas blta ba ma la sogs//mdzes ma rnam+sa
39b1(rgyud): ngag tsan te//sangs rgyas mchod sogs thams cad du//pu ri'i btung ba mthung 'gyur dang//bskyed mos tshal dang rdzing bu'i 'gram//bsti gnas 'gro ba la sogs kyis//tshim 
39b2(rgyud): dang sto mo 'dod pa ltar//bla ma'i gsung ni bde chen po//mang dang mi mo'i skyeg pa dang//rgod dang sgeg pa'i bya ba gang//mkhas pa rnams kyis yid dam pa//skye bo dbang phyug nyid du'o//

39b5(rgyud): ba skyed pa dang//dbang po gnyis kyis bdag nyid skyes//sbyin sogs pha rol phyin rnams kyis//las kyis skyid pa btsal bya dang//byams dang snying rjes dga' ba dang//
39b6(rgyud): kun gyi mtha' mar gtang snyoms dang//ye shes rnam shes smra ba dang//dbu ma'i tshul ni bzhag pa las//kun la dbugs dbyung sbyin pa dang//skur ba 'debs pa dgag ba dang/
39b7(rgyud): dge ba bcu'i las dang ni//klog dang kha don mchog las//byang chub lam la gnas pa yi//dpa' bo gnyis med lta ba'i phyir//lus la ltos pa med pa yis//grong dang
40a1(rgyud): @#/ O /grong du gnas rnams kyi//de ltar gnas skabs cig pa'o//gnyis pa slar yang bshad par bya//sangs rgyas rnams kyi rgyud la dga'//don dam
40a2(rgyud): rnams la gsal ba yis//rnal 'byor ma rnams kyi gnasu//dngos grub gtam rnams brjod pa dang//chos ni don chen 'du shes la//dus gsum 'du shes 
40a3(rgyud): bsgom pa'o//'gro drug yong ba'i 'du shes ni/de nyid rtogs pa'i ming can no//dngos rnams sgyu ma'i 'du shes dang//zhi sogs las kyis bde ba dang//pha dang bu las

40a7(rgyud): smon lam rdzogs ldan la sogs la//rdzogs ldan dus su rang bzhin skye//dga' ba sangs rgyas rdzogs ldan gang//sems can gsum ldan la phra skyes//rig pa de 
40b1(rgyud): la 'ang sboms po skyes//gnyis ldan la ni bde ba phyed//brtsod dus cha'i rags pa'o//thams cad sangs rgyas yon tan thob//smon lam gyis ni rang gi 'bras//de ltar bde

40b6(rgyud): yid can rnams kyis grangs brgyad de//'gro ba 'pho 'gyur de bzhin du//skyug 'gyur gnyid yur 'gyur de bzhin//ltung 'gyur 'dzag 'gyur dang ni 'dir//g.yo 'gyur 'gyel bar 'gyur
40b7(rgyud): ba nyid//rnal 'byor ma brgyad dkar mo sogs//bde ba'i rgyu'i gnas skabaso//de ltar gnas skabs rnam bzhi las//khyi gdong ming gi 'du shes 'gyur//yang dag dran pa'i go 'phang+sa
41a1(rgyud): @#/ O /'dir//gang dang su yir gnas par 'gyur//yang dag rtsol ba la gnas nas//khyim ni gzhan gyi grong du'o//sngags bden sbyor ba'i bdag nyid de//dkyil 
41a2(rgyud): 'khor dbusu sgom pa'o//oM ba badzra DA shrA ma na sa a ka s+yA Da ya o taM hUM ka hU Da pha ma Da sa Da swA pa oM Ta swAhA//sum bcu rtsa bdun bdag nyid sngags//shes rab 
41a3(rgyud): thabs te dngos grub ste//kha dog dmar mo drag chen mo//lhag ma dum skyes ma dang mtshungs//chu stod kar ka DA yugs sa//rang gi bdag por rab gnas ma//nye ba'i 
41a4(rgyud): gling du gnas ma ste//sems can rang rang zhing du skyes//dbang gi las lha mo ste//lha dang lha min sogs sngags rig//bdud rtsi lnga dang sgron ma che//mchod pa las rnams

41b1(rgyud): nyung ba rgya cher bshad par bya//dngos po kun la rab 'jug dang//dngos po de las bzlog pa'o//phag mo rengs pa'i las rnams la//mi shes ri bo 'joms pa mo//su
41b2(rgyud): khab ni rdo rje'i gdong de yang//skud pa bzhin du kun 'gro ma//byang chub sems kyis dga' chen po//ka keng rus bdud rtsi sbyin pa mo//de rang bzhin 'dod chags sogs kun/
41b3(rgyud): bzhi pa las 'das mi 'gyur ma//de nas lha mo chen mo brgyad//mngal sems bskyed pa'i mtshan nyid de//thog mar bya rog bzhin sa bon//ma yi pad ma la lhung pa'o//
41b4(rgyud): nub gcig gis ni phye ba yis//phyi gnas 'ug par gnas dang 'dra//zla ba gcig tu gnas nas ni//khyi yi dbugs ni 'phel ba las//phag tu 'ang yan lag bdag nyid can/

41b6(rgyud): dag pa yi btsol ba 'di//gzhan gyi lta ba nya sogs kyis//'jug pa thams cad bden pa ste//'jug med de dngos spyod yul lo//rkang gnyis rkang bzhi la sogs pa'i//rtsol
41b7(rgyud): ba'i yan lag la 'dzeg ma'o//mthong ba dang ni reg pas kyang //'jug med de nyid rtag tu'o//kha dog ser mo grags chen mo//lhag ma gzugs can ma dang mtshungs//rdo rje dkyil
42a1(rgyud): @#/ O /'khor dbus su ni//shes rab thabs kyi rang bzhin can//seng ge 'gro bzhin chu srin la//nye ba'i gling du gnas pa mo//sngags las bzhengs pa'i lha mo rnams/
42a2(rgyud): rang rang zhing dang nye zhing ma'o////oM ba dra dzra shwA sU ka ka rA le s+yA gra ye mu hUM//kha hUM b+hi pha Sha DA hUM hA pha a Ta phaT swAhA//sum bcu rtsa bdun bdag nyid kyi//gtso mo mchog gi 
42a3(rgyud): rnal 'byor ma//dkyil 'khor thig gdab lasogs ni//bsgoms pa'i sbyor ba ma yin te//thig chen gnyis ni gdab nas su//shar dang so sor 'dod thig dang//zur gyi thig 
42a4(rgyud): gnyis gtab byas nas//phur bu yang dag gdab bya ste//tshangs pa'i thig gi logs nas ni//sum bcu rtsa gnyis pa gnyis gdab//sgo ni 'khor lo'i brgyad cha ste//lte ba'i
42a5(rgyud): pad ma de dang mnyam//snying po 'dab ma'i ngos nas ni//cha gsum du yang de shes nas//de'i cha gcig 'khor lo gsum//lhag ma mtshon gyis sa gzhi'o//de'i sum cha phreng ba 
42a6(rgyud): ste//rdo rje pad ma dang 'khor lo//rtsibs rnams 'khor lo dbus ma las//mtshan te gnyis gcig sdom pas sbom//ka ba rnams ni kun tu sbyin//brgyad gsum 'khor lo 
42a7(rgyud): gsum du'o//sgo las gsum 'gyur rta babs te//sgo khyud sgo'i cha can no//stegs bu rnams ni de'i phyed te//'gram dang logs ni de bzhin no//gzungs kyi snam bu dang ni 'dir//rin chen 
42b1(rgyud): phreng phyed snam bu'o//mgo bo'i phreng ba de'i phyed//mun pa'i snam bu de bzhin no//ka ba'i rtse las ma thon rtsamo//sna tshogs rdo rje 'dod chen po//dril chung dra ba gong rnams dang//ba
42b2(rgyud): dan dril bu sgra sgrogs pa'o//de'i phyi chu skyes thams cad du//snying pa lasogs yang dag bris//sna tshogs rdo rje phreng dang 'dir//lhag ma 'bar ba'i phreng ba'o//de'i 
42b3(rgyud): pha rol 'khor yug ni//sa bzhi rim pas bri bar bya//gri gug phreng ba sbyin bya zhing //mgo dang lag la pa dag de bzhin//snying po pad ma'i pha rol du//rin chen phreng

54a1(rgyud): @#/ O /bcu drug bdag nyid dang //nyams dga' 'dur khrod so gnyis na'o//gri khug rin chen rdo rje dang //pad ma 'khor lo ral gri dang //sna tshogs

54a3(rgyud): rgyu yi rdo rje yongs gyur pa//lnga brgya bzhi yis lhag pa yi//dkyil 'khor 'khor lo rdzogs pa'o//rtag tu glu ma'i sgra yis so//mngal bdag nyi ma re rer 'gro ba 'gro
54a4(rgyud): 'bod na//khyod ni ci'i phyir na stong pa nyid du bzhugs//bzhengs shig bdag ni snying rje'i rang bzhin te//rdo rje 'dzin pas bde chen bdag la 'dod pa mdzod//gson 
54a5(rgyud): cig gson cig gzhan gyi dgongs pa nub//'jig rten phyugs gang shi ba bzhin du 'gyur//bdag gi pad+ma rgyas pa la 'dod mdzod//de ltar 'jig rten kun bder 'gyur

56a1(rgyud): @#/ O /skye sa ma dang gzugs can ma//rab gtum ma dang gtum mig dang//'o da chen ma dang sna chen mo//dpa' blo ma dang mi 'u thung ma//lang ka'i dbang mo shing

82a1(rgyud): @#/ O /nyi ma'i gdan gyis gsal byas pa'o//nub phyogs kyi ni gzhi pa dang //rlung gi gnyis pa'i yi ge la//byang gi gnyis pa'i gdan can no//bden bral rdo rje sems

82a3(rgyud): zhe drug pa'o//thog ma'i yi ge'i re mig ni//dgu pas mnan pa mdzes pa'o//gsum pa lnga pa bdun pa la//dum bu gnyis pas sbyar ba'o//ra yig ldan dang dbang ldan chu

109b3(rgyud): kyang ni yi ge 'dra//'og dang steng du 'du byas n+sa//rnal 'byor rnal 'byor ma (ga)nyis med//skad cig bzhi'i bden rnams kyis//de nyid dga' ba bzhi de bzhin//sde ba bzhi dang 'khor lo bzhi/
109b4(rgyud): e baM ma yA sku rnams ni//rgyud 'di mdo ru bstan pa ste//rgyas bshad gzhan du rtog+sa dran bya//mi'u thung ma yi rab sbyor b+sa//cho ga sngag+sa pas shes par bya//namakha' dang mnyam sgo gleg sre ma/
109b5(rgyud): mkha' sbyor ma ru spyod pa rang//ngag kyang skad cig rang bzhin bdag//'gog pa nye bar 'gro bar gyis//gsang chen sems dang sems byung rnams//mtha' ma gsal bar thob pa yin//rin chin 
109b6(rgyud): rdo rje las dga'i gzugs/bltas pa'i 'tshams su mthong bar 'gyur//thams cad bden pa'i sbyor bdag nyid//bzhi po bzhi yi bdag nyid can//ra me sha ra da dbang por//mchog tu rmad byung sh+se
109b7(rgyud): par bya//'dod chag+sa chag+sa bral rnams bsres pa//de yi skad cig las byung min//'byung pa lnga yi bdag nyid las//me yi dbusu bdud rtsi gnas//dbugs kyi 'khor lo rtag tu de'i//de bzhin gash+ye
110a1(rgyud): @#/ O /pa gza' bar bya//lha dang klu rnams bud med la//mkhas pa yis ni rol ba yin//de rnam+sa kyis ni dus kun du//lus can kun gyi 'pho ba yang//sgo dgu'i bdag nyid namakha' la//de rnam+sa bzhi 
110a2(rgyud): 'gyur shes par bya//de ltar sgo gleg+sa gnas rnams ni//rtag tu sum bcu rtsa drug 'gyur //sgo yi dbye ba mi shes la//skyon dang yon tan ji ltar lags//'pho ba'i dus tshod kyi ni tshad//yang dag rab tu sbyor ba
110a3(rgyud): nyon//mdzes pa'i gnas ni lam bzang pos//lam ngan gyis ni srid pa'i skyon//thig le lte ba steng rnams dang//mig dang sna dang rna ba dang//bshang ba dang ni lci ba'i sgo//sgo dgu yi ni mtshan nyid do//
110a4(rgyud): lte bas 'dod pa'i mtho ris dang//thig l+se gzugs kyi lus can rnams//steng gi steng gi gnas kyi ni//'gro dang 'ong rab rab tu grags//gnod sbyin rnams ni sna las so//rna ba dag ni grub pa'i lha//
110a5(rgyud): gal te mig nas ye shes bgrod//mi rnams mi bdag bsgyur rnams 'gyur//srid pa'i sgo yis yid btags rnam+sa//lci b+sa dud 'gro de bzhino//dmyal ba brgyad kyi dngos rnams kyis//lus 
110a6(rgyud): can rnam+sa ni bshad pa las//gzhan gyi sgo ni gsum gsum ste//mkhas pas rtsa yi kun spyod can//nyi shu rtsa bdun rnam+sa kyang ni//smin drug la sogs rgyu skar ro//rnam dag ye shes gsum po ni//
110a7(rgyud): re re'i sgom rnams kyi'o//brtson 'grus dbang po'i dngos rnams kyis//tham+sa cad nyer sbyor mtshan nyid do//sum bcu bdun bdag nyid dbusu//sngon gyi mdog can sgom par bya//rdo rje 
110a8(rgyud): dkar mos gos sa bon//sgo glegs gnasu gzhag par bya//shes rab thab+sa bdag nyid lha mo//sa yi 'khor lo'i sbyor bso//sngag+sa kyi yi ge 'gyur ba ni//oM ma bi tri ka ga Ta Na mu pa kha ra 
110b1(rgyud): hI pu ha ra ri ba ma ya n+ha hUM ya hUM hUM pha hUM Ta pha pha Ta pha swA Ta hA swA hA//oM kha rba ri ye hUM hUM phaT phaT swA hA//de ltar sngags pas yang dag byung //don dam sbyor las skyes pa'o//dpa' bo thams cad mnyam sbyor
110b2(rgyud): las//rdo rje sems dpa' bde ba'i mchog/rdo rje mkha'agro de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan bdag pos 'di gsungaso//zhes bya ba ni//dpal mkha'agro rgya mtsho rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen
110b3(rgyud): po las//mi'u thung ma'i yi ge'i brda'i mtshan nyid kyi rang bzhin gyi ye shes zhe bya ba'i le'u ste sum bcu rtsa gcig pa'o////slar yang mchod pa byas nas ni//yang dang yang du zhus pa nyid//dngos po ye
110b4(rgyud): shes kyi phyag rgya//bcom ldan gtso bo ji ltar lags//bdag gi mtshan nyid bshad bya yis//yid ni gcig du gyur pas nyon//lhan skyes bde dang stong pa dang //rang gis rig dang bde ba che//chos kyi sku dang 
110b5(rgyud): longs spyod rdzogs//sprul pa dang ni rmad byung che//'od gsal sems dang bsam pa dang //zhi dang myang 'das sbyor skyes dang //pha rol phyin dang gnyis med dang //thar pa dang ni don
110b6(rgyud): dam dang //ye shes rnam shes byang chub dang //chos dbyings grong khyer de bzhin du//pad+ma dang ni chos 'byung dang //slar yang bdag med mtho ris dang //zhi dang sgra dang bla ma dang //rig
110b7(rgyud): dang 'khrul med rtog spangs dang //bcud len dang ni smra gcad de//mkhas pas ts+tshu ma'i rnams grangaso//ji ltar de rnams yid dang po//thams cad lan ni ts+tshum ma'i yin//ji ltar yi ge de bzhin don//
110b8(rgyud): dmyal ba la sdang shes par bya//lang ka'i dbang phyug ma'i sbyor bar//rnal 'byor pa khyod kyis bya'o//'phyang bar gyur pa'i sa bon gyi//der ni ts+tshum ma thams cad do//ge sar ye shes khaMs kyi ni//rang gi rang bzhin
111a1(rgyud): @#/ O /du mtshan nyid//rang yode mnyam rnal 'byor pa//pad+ma'i snying po ru ni gnas//ri ni rnam shes 'gro ba ste//yid kyis khams thaMd du'o//lang kar bdag nyid skye bas te//
111a2(rgyud): dngos po'i ye shes rnams kyis byung//dbang phyug 'byung po thams cad kyi//ye shes min pa dngos kun skyes//de pi ko Ta'i gnas su te//slar yang byang chub dran dbang po//gang du ye sh+se 
111a3(rgyud): bsam med pa//der ni bstan pa thob par 'gyur//'dod chags bral bas gang bdag gis//bi ni kun 'gro'i ye shes so//rlung rnams kyi ni mkhar rnams su//dbang po rnams 
111a4(rgyud): kyi bde de bzhin//Di ni rdo rje'i nus par grags//yul rnams kyi ni grong du bgrod//dran pa lhan cig skyes ye shes//ngag du yid ni rab brjod pa//der ni dbang bo'i byang chub kyang//
111a5(rgyud): shes nas rdo rje'i tshul bdag nyid//de ltar de nyid ngas shes nas//rang gi rang bzhin du gnas bya//chos dang 'jig rten bcas pa yang//cung zad ming med bzung bar bya//
111a6(rgyud): yang na dngos po kun bdag nyid//yang na thams cad rnam par spangs//dngos med dngos po can yod min//dngos po med pa gsal mthong 'gyur//dngos dang dngos med rnam par

111a8(rgyud): sum bcu bdun bdag nyid dbus su//sngon gyi gzugs can bsgom par bya//thams cad thabs dang ldan par 'gyur//sngags kyi yige las byung ba'o//oM hUM sa hUM hA /

111b2(rgyud): byed pa yin//las thams cad kyi rang bzhin ni//brgya la na yang gzhan du min//dngos grub ri'i min can ni//nyi shu rtsa brgyad sman du'o//smin drug dang ni snar mar 'gyur//
111b3(rgyud): mgo bo dang ni de bzhin lag/nam so dang ni rgyal nyid dang //skag dang mchu dang gzhan pa ni//shar gyi bre dang byang gi dgo//me bzhi nag pa sa ri dang //sa ga dang ni lha laM 'tsham+sa dang /
111b4(rgyud): snon dang snubs dang chu bstod dang //chu smad dang ni byi bzhin dang //gro bzhin mon gru de bzhin du//mon gre dang ni khrums stod dang //khrums smad dang ni nam gru dang //tha skar de bzhin 
111b5(rgyud): bra nye dang //zla ba nyi ma ra hu la//sa'i ma nyid dang spen pa rnams//rang rang dus su shes par bya//the tshom med pas bzung bas so//thams cad zla ba'i spyod 
111b6(rgyud): bdag nyid//nam mkha' la ni mnyam par gnas//'chad bzhin pa'i rab sbyor bas//sngags pas cho ga shes par bya//dpa' bo thams cad mnyam sbyor las//

112a2(rgyud): bsgrub pos dngos grub thob par 'gyur//zla ba nyi ma gza' bzung sar//rnal 'byor ma rtags mtshan nyid de//po tang gi ni bsod bor smra ba ste//pra ti po tang gi ni lan 
112a3(rgyud): yin no//ga mu 'gro'o lum pa ni//'ong ngo zhes ni bya nyid//de hi grong khyer ro tsa T+ya ka zhes bya//zung shig pa bo hri da ya ni dpa' bo yin//ko t+thA ka ni dril bu'o a li byed pa'o//
112a4(rgyud): shi ra 'khyil ba'i skra yin te//sra ba rna ba'o gzhan pa ni//man tha na ni bdud rtsi ste//na ra phrad pa'o ta li ka yang//mkha'agro ma'o na ra kaM dkyil 'khor//a mu ka ni dur 
112a5(rgyud): khrod ste//kA khi khA sgo'i swAng sa nam//bram ze'o pa ri rgyal po'i rigs//ba ra ti rje'u rigs kru ra ni//rmangs rigs so an ta gdol pa can//a li ka phug+sa Ta ki nI//sring ma 
112a6(rgyud): mu ta ka tshil kra//lce yis reg dge de bzhin du//zas kyis rtogs par rig par bya//kren dri yis dgos pa ste//a ga man zhes bya gnas gang gnas//che ge mo'i shi ra na me tog go/
112a7(rgyud): khab pa lto ba dan ta rgod pa ste//ni ro rdo char ro bi dz+nya pa ti tshim pa'o//rtu ma zhes byas sprin de bzhin//tu ma pra ya ri bo de//sa bu sa ri ta chu rlung //a gu tsha khya ni cha shes te//

112b2(rgyud): reg pa zos pa yin//so la reg pa tshim pa yin//hri ka ngo tsha stong pa la reg pa//'khrig pa'o bla la reg pa ni//de ltar de ltar gyis zhes bar//phyogs mthun pa ru shes par bya//

112b4(rgyud): bzhin gyis//ye shes bya ba'i rang bzhin bdag/bskyed pa srid pa'i sbyor ba dang //sbyor bral kun rdzob de bzhin du//sgyu ma mthun pa 'khor ba dang //

113a2(rgyud): zhu ba'i dngos po ye shes 'dir//rnal 'byor bdud rtsi kun can te//smin rgyu dang ni 'dra ba'i gzugs//bdag nyid gzugs can mthong bar 'gyur//ye shes shin tu bas 
113a3(rgyud): g.yogs pa//kun du 'gro ba'i lhar dran bya//gang 'dra'i snying rje gnyis byar med//de 'dra'i dngos po skye dran bya//sum bcu bdun bdag nyid dbus su//sngon spyan grags 
113a4(rgyud): pa bsgom par bya//gsang sngags bden pa'i gzugs bdag nyid du/chu yi dkyil dbus son ba'o//oM sa marba hA b+hu sha saM na tra pri saM ya na ya ka hUM ra hUM ya pha hUM Ta hUM 
113a5(rgyud): pha pha Ta Ta swA pha ha Ta swAhA//oM druM ma ts+tsha ye hUM hUM phaT phaT swAhA//de ltar sngags gzugs bdag nyid du//kun du 'gro ba shes par bya//mchod pa rdo rje'i rang bzhin du//
113a6(rgyud): lhan skyes mdzes ma 'jas pa'i//ye shes mkha'agro ma dngos bdag/rnal 'byor namakha' gtso bo yin//dbugs ni spa ba byas nas ni//slar yang dbu rgyan kun du 'gro/
113a7(rgyud): sa gsum po'i rigasu ni//gzugs kyi gtso mo bsgom par bya//yid ni nang du chud pa yis//'dod pa'i dngos grub bsgom par bya//slar yang rdo rje phag mo ni//rnal 'byor ma yis

114a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ba dang mnyam dri//ud bal sngon po'i mdog can no//ud pal sngon 'dab ljang khu la//dri ni de nyid kyis mtshon bya//ma l+li ka dang ud pal dri//mu men dang ni mtshung+sa ma'o//
114a2(rgyud): ke ta ka'i dri zhes bya//dmar po ban du ka lta bu'o//pa ta la'i ma li kA'i dri//slar yang namakha'i kha dog go/de ltar las kyi phyag rgya 'gyur//chos kyi phyag rgya bshad par bya//bde gshegs tshog+sa 
114a3(rgyud): pa la dga' ba//mthar skyes rnal 'byor ma mchog yin//bsam gtan bzlas la shin tu dga'//bram ze mo ru rab tu bshad//rtag tu khrus dang gtsang sbra dga'//rje'u rigs kyi bud med du/
114a4(rgyud): rnam rtog med bdag nyid mi mo//brtul cing dba' bo la rtsi zhing //rgyal rigs kyi ni rang bzhin te//'di ni mchog tu gtam dga' ma'o//las la yongasu rtse ba ste//rmangs rigs kyi ni rnal 'byor

114a6(rgyud): sha ni za ma yin//bde bar gshegs pa'i lam bsrung ma//byang chub sems dpa'i bsam pa byed//khro ba'i gnas la mi khro zhing//mnyes par byed pa'i khur khyer ma'o//de ltar chos kyi phyag rgya ste//
114a7(rgyud): dam tshig phyag rgya mngon brjod bya//gtor ma mchod pa la dga' ma//dam tshig can du de mngon brjod//rab gnas dkyil 'khor lo dang//don dam pa la dga' ba mo//'gro ba rnams la dbang 
114a8(rgyud): bskur ma//rnal 'byor dam tshig can mar grags//chos kyi 'khor lo rab bskor zhing//rtag tu dam tshig skyong ma'o//rnal 'byor ma'i tshogs dbusu//mchog tu rmad byung rtse na ma'o//dus thaMd 
114b1(rgyud): du dga' dang bcas//rab tu rgyas pa'i bzhin byed ma'o//rnal 'byor pa rnams bu bzhin snang //rdo rje rnal 'byor ma de shes//dus kun du ni dub med pa//mtshon cha 'bebs ming can ma'o//

114b7(rgyud): can gzugs ni des de bzhin//zhing du ka la sogs pa'i gzugs/chen po'i sngags kyi tshul dag tu//sa la da lta nyid rol 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun gyi rigs la yang //gzugs dang gzugs med shes mi 'gyur /
114b8(rgyud): gzhan gyi gzugs su byas lha mo//sangs rgyas lam ni shes byed ma'o//dbang po kun du 'gro sangs rgyas//gang phyir shes rab pha rol phyin//de ltar de nyid nges shes pas//rnal 'byor ma'i 
115a1(rgyud): @#/ O /gzugs dang mtshungs//ye shes 'khor lo lha mo rnams//bdag mo 'dod pa kun sbyin ma//dkyil 'khor 'khor lo'i dbusu ni//sngags kyi gzug+sa 
115a2(rgyud): can du byung ba'o//oM dza ma Ta hA ka kA lA ya bA man+ta ku ga tA ni ya te hUM dzra hUM ya phaT hUM swA pa hA Ta phaT swAhA//oM ai ra ba ti ye hUM hUM phaT phaT swAhA//zhes bya'i sngags kyi sa b+no

115a4(rgyud): ba'i mchog/rdo rje mkha'agro de bzhin gshegs//bcom ldan bdag pos 'di gsungs so//dpal mkha'agro rgya mtsho rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po las/sa srung 
115a5(rgyud): ma'i sku'i phyag rgya'i mtshan nyid kyi cho ga dang ldan pa zhes bya ste le'u sum bcu rtsa gcig pa'o//de nas lha mos mchod byas nas//'di skad kyi ni tshig smras so//gang gi 'byung po 
115a6(rgyud): thams cad sogs//mthong bas kun du skrag pa yin//thams cad du ni bden pa'i brda//bcom ldan mkha'agro nyid kyis gsungs//phyag rgya kun gyi nang son pa'i//
